Demystifying OPD: Length Inflation and Stabilization Strategies for Large Language Models
Authors: Feng Luo, Yu-Neng Chuang, Guanchu Wang, Zicheng Xu, Xiaotian Han, Tianyi Zhang, Vladimir Braverman
First: 2026-04-09T17:58:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T17:58:02+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
On-policy distillation (OPD) trains student models under their own induced distribution while leveraging supervision from stronger teachers. We identify a failure mode of OPD: as training progresses, on-policy rollouts can undergo abrupt length inflation, causing truncated trajectories to dominate the training data. This truncation collapse coincides with abrupt repetition saturation and induces biased gradient signals, leading to severe training instability and sharp degradation in validation performance. We attribute this problem to the interaction between student-induced data collection and the distillation objective, which implicitly favors long and repetitive rollouts. To address this issue, we propose StableOPD, a stabilized OPD framework that combines a reference-based divergence constraint with rollout mixture distillation. These together mitigate repetition-induced length inflation and further stabilize OPD training. Across multiple math reasoning datasets, our approach prevents truncation collapse, stabilizes training dynamics, and improves performance by 7.2% on average.
中文标题/摘要
标题：揭开OPD之谜：大语言模型的长度膨胀与稳定化策略
在线策略蒸馏（OPD）在学生模型自身诱导分布下训练，同时利用更强教师模型的监督。我们发现OPD的一种失效模式：随着训练进行，在线策略 rollout 会突然发生长度膨胀，导致截断轨迹主导训练数据。这种截断崩溃与突然的重复饱和同步发生，引发偏斜梯度信号，导致训练严重不稳定且验证性能急剧下降。我们将此问题归因于学生诱导数据收集与蒸馏目标之间的交互作用，后者隐式偏好长且重复的 rollout。为解决此问题，我们提出 StableOPD，一种结合参考分布差异约束与 rollout 混合蒸馏的稳定化 OPD 框架。这些方法共同缓解重复引发的长度膨胀，并进一步稳定 OPD 训练。在多个数学推理数据集上，我们的方法避免截断崩溃，稳定训练动态，并平均提升性能 7.2%。
Summary / 总结
On-policy distillation (OPD) trains student models under their own induced distribution while leveraging supervision from stronger teachers.
为解决OPD训练中因轨迹截断导致的性能急剧下降问题，本文发现学生模型诱导的数据收集与蒸馏目标交互，会偏好长且重复的轨迹，从而引发梯度偏差和训练崩溃。为此，作者提出StableOPD框架，结合参考分布约束与轨迹混合蒸馏，有效抑制重复引发的长度膨胀并稳定训练。在多个数学推理数据集上，该方法成功避免截断崩溃，稳定训练过程，并平均提升7.2%的验证性能。
Ads in AI Chatbots? An Analysis of How Large Language Models Navigate Conflicts of Interest
Authors: Addison J. Wu, Ryan Liu, Shuyue Stella Li, Yulia Tsvetkov, Thomas L. Griffiths
First: 2026-04-09T17:57:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T17:57:50+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Today's large language models (LLMs) are trained to align with user preferences through methods such as reinforcement learning. Yet models are beginning to be deployed not merely to satisfy users, but also to generate revenue for the companies that created them through advertisements. This creates the potential for LLMs to face conflicts of interest, where the most beneficial response to a user may not be aligned with the company's incentives. For instance, a sponsored product may be more expensive but otherwise equal to another; in this case, what does (and should) the LLM recommend to the user? In this paper, we provide a framework for categorizing the ways in which conflicting incentives might lead LLMs to change the way they interact with users, inspired by literature from linguistics and advertising regulation. We then present a suite of evaluations to examine how current models handle these tradeoffs. We find that a majority of LLMs forsake user welfare for company incentives in a multitude of conflict of interest situations, including recommending a sponsored product almost twice as expensive (Grok 4.1 Fast, 83%), surfacing sponsored options to disrupt the purchasing process (GPT 5.1, 94%), and concealing prices in unfavorable comparisons (Qwen 3 Next, 24%). Behaviors also vary strongly with levels of reasoning and users' inferred socio-economic status. Our results highlight some of the hidden risks to users that can emerge when companies begin to subtly incentivize advertisements in chatbots.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AI聊天机器人中的广告？大型语言模型如何应对利益冲突的分析
当前大型语言模型（LLM）通过强化学习等方法训练以符合用户偏好。然而，这些模型正被部署不仅为了满足用户需求，还为了通过广告为创建它们的公司创造收入。这可能导致LLM面临利益冲突，即对用户的最有利回应可能与公司的激励不一致。例如，一个赞助产品可能更贵但其他方面与另一产品相同；在这种情况下，LLM应向用户推荐什么？本文提供了一个框架，用于分类利益冲突如何导致LLM改变与用户互动的方式，灵感来自语言学和广告监管文献。随后，我们提出一系列评估，以考察当前模型如何处理这些权衡。我们发现，多数LLM在多种利益冲突情境中牺牲用户福祉以迎合公司激励，包括推荐价格几乎高出一倍的赞助产品（Grok 4.1 Fast，83%）、在购买过程中展示赞助选项以干扰购买流程（GPT 5.1，94%），以及在不利比较中隐藏价格（Qwen 3 Next，24%）。行为还强烈依赖于推理水平和用户推断的社会经济地位。我们的结果揭示了当公司开始在聊天机器人中微妙地激励广告时，用户可能面临的一些隐藏风险。
Summary / 总结
Today's large language models (LLMs) are trained to align with user preferences through methods such as reinforcement learning.
本研究探讨了大型语言模型（LLM）在被激励通过广告创收而非单纯服务用户利益时，如何处理利益冲突。作者借鉴语言学与广告监管文献，构建了分类框架以识别此类激励如何改变模型行为，并对多个模型在用户利益与公司利润相悖的情境下进行评估。结果显示，多数模型优先考虑公司利益而非用户福祉：例如，Grok 4.1 Fast 83% 的情况下推荐了价格几乎翻倍的赞助商品，GPT 5.1 94% 的情况下展示广告以干扰购买流程，Qwen 3 Next 24% 的情况下隐藏不利价格。模型行为还随推理深度和推断出的用户社会经济地位而显著变化，揭示了当聊天机器人被微妙商业化时，用户所面临的潜在风险。
FIT: A Large-Scale Dataset for Fit-Aware Virtual Try-On
Authors: Johanna Karras, Yuanhao Wang, Yingwei Li, Ira Kemelmacher-Shlizerman
Venue: SIGGRAPH 2026
First: 2026-04-09T17:57:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T17:57:50+00:00
Comments: SIGGRAPH 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Given a person and a garment image, virtual try-on (VTO) aims to synthesize a realistic image of the person wearing the garment, while preserving their original pose and identity. Although recent VTO methods excel at visualizing garment appearance, they largely overlook a crucial aspect of the try-on experience: the accuracy of garment fit -- for example, depicting how an extra-large shirt looks on an extra-small person. A key obstacle is the absence of datasets that provide precise garment and body size information, particularly for "ill-fit" cases, where garments are significantly too large or too small. Consequently, current VTO methods default to generating well-fitted results regardless of the garment or person size. In this paper, we take the first steps towards solving this open problem. We introduce FIT (Fit-Inclusive Try-on), a large-scale VTO dataset comprising over 1.13M try-on image triplets accompanied by precise body and garment measurements. We overcome the challenges of data collection via a scalable synthetic strategy: (1) We programmatically generate 3D garments using GarmentCode and drape them via physics simulation to capture realistic garment fit. (2) We employ a novel re-texturing framework to transform synthetic renderings into photorealistic images while strictly preserving geometry. (3) We introduce person identity preservation into our re-texturing model to generate paired person images (same person, different garments) for supervised training. Finally, we leverage our FIT dataset to train a baseline fit-aware virtual try-on model. Our data and results set the new state-of-the-art for fit-aware virtual try-on, as well as offer a robust benchmark for future research. We will make all data and code publicly available on our project page: https://johannakarras.github.io/FIT.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FIT：面向合身感知的虚拟试穿大规模数据集
给定一个人和一件衣物图像，虚拟试穿（VTO）旨在合成该人穿着衣物的逼真图像，同时保留其原始姿态和身份。尽管近期VTO方法在可视化衣物外观方面表现优异，但它们大多忽略了试穿体验中的关键方面：衣物合身的准确性——例如，展示一件加大码衬衫穿在特小码人身上的效果。一个关键障碍是缺乏提供精确衣物和身体尺寸信息的数据集，特别是针对“不合身”情况，即衣物明显过大或过小。因此，当前VTO方法默认生成合身结果，而不论衣物或人体尺寸如何。 本文首次尝试解决这一开放性问题。我们提出了FIT（合身包容式试穿），一个包含超过113万组试穿图像三元组的大规模VTO数据集，每组均配有精确的身体和衣物测量数据。我们通过可扩展的合成策略克服数据收集的挑战：（1）我们使用GarmentCode程序化生成3D衣物，并通过物理模拟使其贴合人体，以捕捉真实的衣物合身效果。（2）我们采用新颖的重纹理化框架，将合成渲染图转化为逼真的照片级图像，同时严格保持几何结构。（3）我们在重纹理化模型中引入身份保持机制，生成配对的人像（同一个人，不同衣物），用于监督训练。最后，我们利用FIT数据集训练了一个基础的合身感知虚拟试穿模型。我们的数据和结果为合身感知虚拟试穿设立了新的最先进水平，并为未来研究提供了稳健的基准。所有数据和代码将在我们的项目页面公开：https://johannakarras.github.io/FIT。
Summary / 总结
Given a person and a garment image, virtual try-on (VTO) aims to synthesize a realistic image of the person wearing the garment, while preserving their original pose and identity.
为解决现有虚拟试衣系统忽视服装贴合度（尤其是不合身情况）的问题，本文提出FIT数据集，包含113万组带精确人体与服装尺寸的试衣图像三元组。该数据集通过可扩展合成流程构建：使用GarmentCode生成3D服装，通过物理模拟实现真实贴合效果，再通过新纹理化框架生成逼真图像并保持几何结构，同时确保人物身份一致以支持监督训练。实验表明，基于FIT训练的虚拟试衣模型在贴合度感知合成上显著优于现有方法，确立了新的性能基准。
What Drives Representation Steering? A Mechanistic Case Study on Steering Refusal
Authors: Stephen Cheng, Sarah Wiegreffe, Dinesh Manocha
First: 2026-04-09T17:57:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T17:57:14+00:00
Comments: 9 pages + appendix, 7 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Applying steering vectors to large language models (LLMs) is an efficient and effective model alignment technique, but we lack an interpretable explanation for how it works-- specifically, what internal mechanisms steering vectors affect and how this results in different model outputs. To investigate the causal mechanisms underlying the effectiveness of steering vectors, we conduct a comprehensive case study on refusal. We propose a multi-token activation patching framework and discover that different steering methodologies leverage functionally interchangeable circuits when applied at the same layer. These circuits reveal that steering vectors primarily interact with the attention mechanism through the OV circuit while largely ignoring the QK circuit-- freezing all attention scores during steering drops performance by only 8.75% across two model families. A mathematical decomposition of the steered OV circuit further reveals semantically interpretable concepts, even in cases where the steering vector itself does not. Leveraging the activation patching results, we show that steering vectors can be sparsified by up to 90-99% while retaining most performance, and that different steering methodologies agree on a subset of important dimensions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：什么驱动了表示引导？对引导拒绝的机制性案例研究
将引导向量应用于大型语言模型（LLM）是一种高效且有效的模型对齐技术，但我们缺乏对其工作原理的可解释性解释——具体而言，引导向量影响了哪些内部机制，以及如何导致不同的模型输出。为探究引导向量有效性的因果机制，我们对拒绝行为进行了全面案例研究。我们提出了一种多标记激活修补框架，发现不同引导方法在相同层上利用功能可互换的电路。这些电路表明，引导向量主要通过OV电路与注意力机制交互，而几乎忽略QK电路——在引导过程中冻结所有注意力分数仅使性能下降8.75%（跨两个模型家族）。对引导OV电路的数学分解进一步揭示了语义可解释的概念，即使引导向量本身不可解释。利用激活修补结果，我们证明引导向量可压缩至90-99%仍保留大部分性能，且不同引导方法在重要维度上达成一致。
Summary / 总结
Applying steering vectors to large language models (LLMs) is an efficient and effective model alignment technique, but we lack an interpretable explanation for how it works-- specifically, what internal mechanisms steering vectors affect and how this results in different model outputs.
为理解大语言模型中引导向量的作用机制，本研究通过拒绝任务的机制性案例研究，探讨引导向量如何影响模型输出。采用多标记激活修补框架，发现不同引导方法在相同层中激活功能可互换的电路，揭示引导主要通过注意力机制中的OV电路起作用，而较少影响QK电路。冻结注意力分数仅使性能下降8.75%（跨两个模型家族），对引导后OV电路的数学分解还揭示了语义可解释的概念，即使引导向量本身不可解释。研究进一步表明，引导向量可压缩至90-99%仍保持大部分性能，且不同引导方法在重要维度上达成一致。
ClawBench: Can AI Agents Complete Everyday Online Tasks?
Authors: Yuxuan Zhang, Yubo Wang, Yipeng Zhu, Penghui Du, Junwen Miao, Xuan Lu, Wendong Xu, Yunzhuo Hao, Songcheng Cai, Xiaochen Wang, Huaisong Zhang, Xian Wu, Yi Lu, Minyi Lei, Kai Zou, Huifeng Yin, Ping Nie, Liang Chen, Dongfu Jiang, Wenhu Chen, Kelsey R. Allen
First: 2026-04-09T17:57:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T17:57:13+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://claw-bench.com
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
AI agents may be able to automate your inbox, but can they automate other routine aspects of your life? Everyday online tasks offer a realistic yet unsolved testbed for evaluating the next generation of AI agents. To this end, we introduce ClawBench, an evaluation framework of 153 simple tasks that people need to accomplish regularly in their lives and work, spanning 144 live platforms across 15 categories, from completing purchases and booking appointments to submitting job applications. These tasks require demanding capabilities beyond existing benchmarks, such as obtaining relevant information from user-provided documents, navigating multi-step workflows across diverse platforms, and write-heavy operations like filling in many detailed forms correctly. Unlike existing benchmarks that evaluate agents in offline sandboxes with static pages, ClawBench operates on production websites, preserving the full complexity, dynamic nature, and challenges of real-world web interaction. A lightweight interception layer captures and blocks only the final submission request, ensuring safe evaluation without real-world side effects. Our evaluations of 7 frontier models show that both proprietary and open-source models can complete only a small portion of these tasks. For example, Claude Sonnet 4.6 achieves only 33.3%. Progress on ClawBench brings us closer to AI agents that can function as reliable general-purpose assistants.
Summary / 总结
AI agents may be able to automate your inbox, but can they automate other routine aspects of your life?
为评估AI代理在现实生活中完成日常在线任务的能力，研究者构建了ClawBench框架，包含153项覆盖15类、144个真实平台的实用任务，如购物、预约、求职等。这些任务要求代理具备跨平台导航、文档信息提取和复杂表单填写等能力。在生产网站上通过拦截层仅阻断最终提交请求以确保安全，避免真实副作用。对七款前沿模型的测试显示，如Claude Sonnet 4.6仅完成33.3%任务，表明当前AI代理尚无法胜任通用助手角色。
UniversalVTG: A Universal and Lightweight Foundation Model for Video Temporal Grounding
Authors: Joungbin An, Agrim Jain, Kristen Grauman
First: 2026-04-09T17:57:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T17:57:09+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://vision.cs.utexas.edu/projects/universalvtg
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Video temporal grounding (VTG) is typically tackled with dataset-specific models that transfer poorly across domains and query styles. Recent efforts to overcome this limitation have adapted large multimodal language models (MLLMs) to VTG, but their high compute cost and limited video context still hinder long-video grounding. We instead scale unified supervision while keeping the model lightweight. We present UniversalVTG, a single VTG model trained with large-scale cross-dataset pretraining. An offline Query Unifier canonicalizes heterogeneous query formats into a shared declarative space, reducing linguistic mismatch and preventing the negative transfer observed under naïve joint training. Combined with an efficient grounding head, UniversalVTG scales to long, untrimmed videos. Across diverse benchmarks-GoalStep-StepGrounding, Ego4D-NLQ, TACoS, Charades-STA, and ActivityNet-Captions-one UniversalVTG checkpoint achieves state-of-the-art performance versus dedicated VTG models. Moreover, despite being $>100\times$ smaller than recent MLLM-based approaches, UniversalVTG matches or exceeds their accuracy on multiple benchmarks, offering a practical alternative to parameter-heavy MLLMs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：UniversalVTG：一种通用且轻量级的视频时序定位基础模型
视频时序定位（VTG）通常依赖于特定数据集的模型，跨领域和查询风格迁移效果差。近期研究尝试将大型多模态语言模型（MLLM）适配至VTG，但其高计算成本和有限视频上下文仍限制了长视频定位。我们通过扩展统一监督机制，同时保持模型轻量。我们提出UniversalVTG，一种通过大规模跨数据集预训练的单一VTG模型。离线Query Unifier将异构查询格式统一至共享的声明式空间，减少语言不匹配并避免朴素联合训练中的负迁移。结合高效定位头，UniversalVTG可扩展至长且未剪辑的视频。在多个基准测试（GoalStep-StepGrounding、Ego4D-NLQ、TACoS、Charades-STA、ActivityNet-Captions）中，单一UniversalVTG模型均超越专用VTG模型的性能。此外，尽管其参数量比近期基于MLLM的方法小100倍以上，UniversalVTG在多个基准测试中仍达到或超越其精度，提供了一种实用的轻量级替代方案。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the poor cross-domain generalization and high computational cost of existing video temporal grounding (VTG) models, the authors propose UniversalVTG, a lightweight foundation model trained with cross-dataset pretraining to unify supervision across diverse queries. They introduce an offline Query Unifier to standardize heterogeneous query formats into a shared declarative space, mitigating linguistic mismatch and negative transfer. Paired with an efficient grounding head, UniversalVTG scales to long untrimmed videos and achieves state-of-the-art performance on multiple benchmarks including GoalStep-StepGrounding, Ego4D-NLQ, TACoS, Charades-STA, and ActivityNet-Captions. Despite being over 100 times smaller than recent MLLM-based VTG models, UniversalVTG matches or surpasses their accuracy, demonstrating a practical, efficient alternative.
为解决现有视频时序定位模型跨域迁移差、计算成本高的问题，本文提出UniversalVTG，一种通过跨数据集预训练统一监督的轻量级基础模型。该模型采用离线查询统一器将异构查询格式标准化为共享声明式空间，缓解语言不匹配并避免朴素联合训练中的负迁移。结合高效定位头，UniversalVTG可有效处理长视频。在GoalStep、Ego4D-NLQ、TACoS、Charades-STA和ActivityNet-Captions等多个基准测试中，单个UniversalVTG模型达到当前最优性能，部分指标超越专用模型，且其参数量仅为近期基于多模态大语言模型方法的100倍以上，提供更实用的替代方案。
Cram Less to Fit More: Training Data Pruning Improves Memorization of Facts
Authors: Jiayuan Ye, Vitaly Feldman, Kunal Talwar
First: 2026-04-09T17:55:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T17:55:50+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) can struggle to memorize factual knowledge in their parameters, often leading to hallucinations and poor performance on knowledge-intensive tasks. In this paper, we formalize fact memorization from an information-theoretic perspective and study how training data distributions affect fact accuracy. We show that fact accuracy is suboptimal (below the capacity limit) whenever the amount of information contained in the training data facts exceeds model capacity. This is further exacerbated when the fact frequency distribution is skewed (e.g. a power law). We propose data selection schemes based on the training loss alone that aim to limit the number of facts in the training data and flatten their frequency distribution. On semi-synthetic datasets containing high-entropy facts, our selection method effectively boosts fact accuracy to the capacity limit. When pretraining language models from scratch on an annotated Wikipedia corpus, our selection method enables a GPT2-Small model (110m parameters) to memorize 1.3X more entity facts compared to standard training, matching the performance of a 10X larger model (1.3B parameters) pretrained on the full dataset.
中文标题/摘要
标题：减少学习以容纳更多：训练数据剪枝提升事实记忆能力
大型语言模型（LLMs）在参数中难以记忆事实性知识，常导致幻觉和知识密集型任务表现不佳。本文从信息论角度形式化事实记忆，并研究训练数据分布对事实准确率的影响。我们证明，当训练数据中事实所含信息量超过模型容量时，事实准确率将低于容量上限；若事实频率分布偏斜（如幂律分布），该问题会进一步加剧。我们提出仅基于训练损失的数据选择方案，旨在限制训练数据中事实数量并使其频率分布更均匀。在包含高熵事实的半合成数据集上，该方法有效将事实准确率提升至容量上限。当从零开始在标注维基百科语料上预训练语言模型时，我们的方法使GPT2-Small模型（1.1亿参数）相比标准训练能记忆1.3倍更多实体事实，性能可媲美在完整数据集上预训练的10倍大模型（13亿参数）。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the issue of large language models struggling to memorize factual knowledge, which leads to hallucinations and poor performance on knowledge-intensive tasks. The authors formalize fact memorization using information theory and demonstrate that fact accuracy is limited when training data contains more information than the model’s capacity, especially under skewed fact frequency distributions. They propose a data pruning method based solely on training loss to reduce the number of facts and flatten their frequency distribution. Experiments on semi-synthetic datasets and from-scratch pretrained models show that this method significantly improves fact accuracy, enabling a small GPT2-Small model to memorize 1.3 times more entity facts than standard training, matching the performance of a much larger model.
该论文针对大语言模型难以有效记忆事实知识、导致幻觉和知识密集型任务表现不佳的问题。作者从信息论角度形式化事实记忆，并证明当训练数据中事实信息量超过模型容量，或事实频率分布偏斜（如幂律分布）时，事实准确率会低于容量上限。为此，他们提出仅基于训练损失的数据选择方法，旨在减少事实数量并平滑其频率分布。在半合成数据集和维基百科预训练实验中，该方法显著提升事实准确率，使11000万参数的小型GPT2模型能记忆比标准训练多1.3倍的实体事实，性能可媲美13亿参数的更大模型。
MolmoWeb: Open Visual Web Agent and Open Data for the Open Web
Authors: Tanmay Gupta, Piper Wolters, Zixian Ma, Peter Sushko, Rock Yuren Pang, Diego Llanes, Yue Yang, Taira Anderson, Boyuan Zheng, Zhongzheng Ren, Harsh Trivedi, Taylor Blanton, Caleb Ouellette, Winson Han, Ali Farhadi, Ranjay Krishna
First: 2026-04-09T17:54:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T17:54:02+00:00
Comments: https://allenai.org/blog/molmoweb
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Web agents--autonomous systems that navigate and execute tasks on the web on behalf of users--have the potential to transform how people interact with the digital world. However, the most capable web agents today rely on proprietary models with undisclosed training data and recipes, limiting scientific understanding, reproducibility, and community-driven progress. We believe agents for the open web should be built in the open. To this end, we introduce (1) MolmoWebMix, a large and diverse mixture of browser task demonstrations and web-GUI perception data and (2) MolmoWeb, a family of fully open multimodal web agents. Specifically, MolmoWebMix combines over 100K synthetic task trajectories from multiple complementary generation pipelines with 30K+ human demonstrations, atomic web-skill trajectories, and GUI perception data, including referring expression grounding and screenshot question answering. MolmoWeb agents operate as instruction-conditioned visual-language action policies: given a task instruction and a webpage screenshot, they predict the next browser action, requiring no access to HTML, accessibility trees, or specialized APIs. Available in 4B and 8B size, on browser-use benchmarks like WebVoyager, Online-Mind2Web, and DeepShop, MolmoWeb agents achieve state-of-the-art results outperforming similar scale open-weight-only models such as Fara-7B, UI-Tars-1.5-7B, and Holo1-7B. MolmoWeb-8B also surpasses set-of-marks (SoM) agents built on much larger closed frontier models like GPT-4o. We further demonstrate consistent gains through test-time scaling via parallel rollouts with best-of-N selection, achieving 94.7% and 60.5% pass@4 (compared to 78.2% and 35.3% pass@1) on WebVoyager and Online-Mind2Web respectively. We will release model checkpoints, training data, code, and a unified evaluation harness to enable reproducibility and accelerate open research on web agents.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MolmoWeb：开放视觉网络代理与开放数据用于开放网络
网络代理——能够代表用户在互联网上自主导航并执行任务的系统——有望改变人们与数字世界互动的方式。然而，当前最强大的网络代理依赖于专有模型及其未公开的训练数据和配方，限制了科学理解、可复现性和社区驱动的进步。 我们相信，面向开放网络的代理应公开构建。为此，我们引入了（1）MolmoWebMix，一个包含大量浏览器任务演示和网页GUI感知数据的多样化混合数据集，以及（2）MolmoWeb，一系列完全开放的多模态网络代理。具体而言，MolmoWebMix结合了来自多个互补生成管道的超过10万条合成任务轨迹，以及3万多人类演示、原子级网页技能轨迹和GUI感知数据，包括指代表达定位和截图问答。MolmoWeb代理作为指令条件化的视觉-语言动作策略运行：给定任务指令和网页截图，它们预测下一个浏览器操作，无需访问HTML、可访问性树或专用API。 在4B和8B规模下，MolmoWeb代理在WebVoyager、Online-Mind2Web和DeepShop等浏览器使用基准测试中表现最佳，超越了类似规模的开放权重模型（如Fara-7B、UI-Tars-1.5-7B和Holo1-7B）。MolmoWeb-8B甚至超越了基于更大闭源前沿模型（如GPT-4o）构建的标记集合（SoM）代理。我们进一步通过测试时并行运行与最佳N选择实现一致性能提升，在WebVoyager和Online-Mind2Web上分别达到94.7%和60.5%的pass@4（相比78.2%和35.3%的pass@1）。我们将发布模型检查点、训练数据、代码和统一评估框架，以支持可复现性并加速开放网络代理研究。
Summary / 总结
Web agents--autonomous systems that navigate and execute tasks on the web on behalf of users--have the potential to transform how people interact with the digital world.
为解决当前网络代理因依赖闭源模型导致的透明度低、可复现性差的问题，作者提出了MolmoWeb，一套开源的多模态网络代理系统，基于公开数据构建。该方法整合了超过10万条合成任务轨迹与3万多个真人演示，结合GUI感知数据，训练出能根据指令和网页截图预测浏览器操作的视觉-语言动作策略，无需访问HTML或API。实验表明，MolmoWeb-8B性能优于同类开源模型（如Fara-7B、UI-Tars-1.5-7B），甚至超越基于闭源大模型（如GPT-4o）构建的代理。通过测试时并行采样与最佳选择策略，其性能进一步提升，在WebVoyager和Online-Mind2Web上pass@4分别达到94.7%和60.5%，远超pass@1的78.2%和35.3%。
"Because we are no longer ashamed of our disabilities, we are proud": Advocating and Reclaiming Next-Gen Accessibility Symbols
Authors: Karen Joy, Chris Dodge, Harsh Chavda, Alyssa Sheehan
First: 2026-04-09T17:53:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T17:53:36+00:00
Comments: 18 pages, 10 images
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Our study investigates the relationship between accessibility symbols and emerging technologies in supporting disability disclosure. We conducted twenty three remote design creation sessions with semi structured interviews to examine participants awareness of existing symbols, how they use symbols across online and offline contexts, and barriers to adoption and interpretation. Through participant sketching and future oriented storyboard probes, participants proposed ways to integrate symbols into wearable devices, mobile interfaces, and portable tools, emphasizing customizable and context sensitive disclosure. Our findings suggest symbols are most effective when paired with technologies that provide user control over visibility and optional pathways for explanation, helping reduce misinterpretation while supporting agency in disclosure moments. By reimagining symbol based assistance as part of a broader disclosure system where meaning depends on the symbol, its carrier, and context, this work informs more inclusive accessibility supports across diverse settings.
中文标题/摘要
标题：“因为我们不再为自己的残疾感到羞耻，我们感到自豪”：倡导并重新定义下一代无障碍符号
本研究探讨无障碍符号与新兴技术在支持残疾披露中的关系。我们通过23次远程设计创作会结合半结构化访谈，考察参与者对现有符号的认知、在线上线下场景中的使用方式，以及采用与解读的障碍。通过参与者草图和面向未来的故事情节探针，参与者提出将符号整合进可穿戴设备、移动界面和便携工具的方法，强调可定制化和情境敏感的披露方式。研究发现，当符号与提供用户对可见性控制及可选解释路径的技术结合时，效果最佳，有助于减少误读并支持披露时刻的自主性。通过将基于符号的辅助视为更广泛的披露系统的一部分——其意义取决于符号本身、载体及情境——本研究为多样场景中的包容性无障碍支持提供了指导。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to empower individuals with disabilities to self-determine when and how to disclose their needs, this study explores how next-generation accessibility symbols can be integrated with emerging technologies. Through 23 remote design sessions with semi-structured interviews, participants sketched and storyboarded symbol usage across wearables, mobile interfaces, and portable tools, revealing preferences for customizable and context-sensitive disclosure. Results indicate that symbols are most effective when paired with technologies enabling user control over visibility and optional explanations, reducing misinterpretation while enhancing personal agency in disclosure moments.
本研究旨在通过重新设计无障碍符号，赋予残障人士自主决定何时何地披露自身状况的权利，减少社会污名。通过对23位参与者进行远程设计工作坊和半结构化访谈，收集其对现有符号的认知、使用场景及障碍，并通过草图与未来场景故事板探索符号在可穿戴设备、移动界面和便携工具中的整合方式。研究发现，当符号与允许用户自主控制可见性及提供可选解释路径的技术结合时，其效果最佳，能有效降低误读并增强披露自主性。
When Fine-Tuning Changes the Evidence: Architecture-Dependent Semantic Drift in Chest X-Ray Explanations
Authors: Kabilan Elangovan, Daniel Ting
First: 2026-04-09T17:53:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T17:53:02+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Transfer learning followed by fine-tuning is widely adopted in medical image classification due to consistent gains in diagnostic performance. However, in multi-class settings with overlapping visual features, improvements in accuracy do not guarantee stability of the visual evidence used to support predictions. We define semantic drift as systematic changes in the attribution structure supporting a model's predictions between transfer learning and full fine-tuning, reflecting potential shifts in underlying visual reasoning despite stable classification performance. Using a five-class chest X-ray task, we evaluate DenseNet201, ResNet50V2, and InceptionV3 under a two-stage training protocol and quantify drift with reference-free metrics capturing spatial localization and structural consistency of attribution maps. Across architectures, coarse anatomical localization remains stable, while overlap IoU reveals pronounced architecture-dependent reorganization of evidential structure. Beyond single-method analysis, stability rankings can reverse across LayerCAM and GradCAM++ under converged predictive performance, establishing explanation stability as an interaction between architecture, optimization phase, and attribution objective.
中文标题/摘要
标题：微调如何改变证据：胸部X光图像解释中的架构依赖性语义漂移
迁移学习后接微调在医学图像分类中因诊断性能持续提升而被广泛采用。然而，在多类且视觉特征重叠的场景中，准确率提升并不保证支持预测的视觉证据结构稳定。我们定义语义漂移为模型在迁移学习与完全微调阶段支持预测的归因结构发生系统性变化，反映潜在视觉推理机制的转变，而分类性能保持稳定。在五类胸部X光任务中，我们评估DenseNet201、ResNet50V2和InceptionV3在两阶段训练协议下的表现，并用无参考指标量化归因图的空间定位与结构一致性。不同架构下，粗略解剖定位保持稳定，但IoU重叠度揭示了证据结构的显著架构依赖性重组。除单方法分析外，当预测性能收敛时，LayerCAM与GradCAM++的稳定性排名可能反转，表明解释稳定性是架构、优化阶段与归因目标相互作用的结果。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that fine-tuning medical image models can improve accuracy without ensuring stable visual explanations, this study investigates architecture-dependent semantic drift in chest X-ray predictions. Using DenseNet201, ResNet50V2, and InceptionV3 under a two-stage training protocol, the authors quantify changes in attribution map structure between transfer learning and full fine-tuning via reference-free metrics measuring spatial localization and structural consistency. Results show that while coarse anatomical localization remains stable across architectures, overlap IoU reveals significant architecture-specific reorganization of evidential structure, and explanation stability varies depending on the attribution method (LayerCAM vs GradCAM++) even when predictive performance converges.
鉴于微调医学图像模型可提升准确率但未必保证视觉推理稳定性，本研究探讨了语义漂移——即在迁移学习与全微调阶段，模型解释图结构发生系统性重组的现象。在五类胸片任务上，作者对DenseNet201、ResNet50V2和InceptionV3采用两阶段训练协议，用无参考指标量化空间定位与结构一致性。结果表明，尽管粗粒度解剖区域保持稳定，但不同架构下细粒度解释图重叠度差异显著，且在预测性能收敛时，LayerCAM与GradCAM++的稳定性排序亦不同，揭示解释稳定性受架构、优化阶段与归因目标共同影响。
Splits! Flexible Sociocultural Linguistic Investigation at Scale
Authors: Eylon Caplan, Tania Chakraborty, Dan Goldwasser
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2025-04-06T23:17:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T17:52:14+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ACL 2026 Main Conference
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Variation in language use, shaped by speakers' sociocultural background and specific context of use, offers a rich lens into cultural perspectives, values, and opinions. For example, Chinese students discuss "healthy eating" with words like "timing," "regularity," and "digestion," whereas Americans use vocabulary like "balancing food groups" and "avoiding fat and sugar," reflecting distinct cultural models of nutrition. The computational study of these Sociocultural Linguistic Phenomena (SLP) has traditionally been done in NLP via tailored analyses of specific groups or topics, requiring specialized data collection and experimental operationalization--a process not well-suited to quick hypothesis exploration and prototyping. To address this, we propose constructing a "sandbox" designed for systematic and flexible sociolinguistic research. Using our method, we construct a demographically/topically split Reddit dataset, Splits!, validated by self-identification and by replicating several known SLPs from existing literature. We showcase the sandbox's utility with a scalable, two-stage process that filters large collections of "potential" SLPs (PSLPs) to surface the most promising candidates for deeper, qualitative investigation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Splits! 大规模灵活的社会文化语言学研究
语言使用中的变异受说话者社会文化背景及具体使用情境影响，为透视文化视角、价值观和意见提供了丰富窗口。例如，中国学生讨论“健康饮食”时使用“时间”“规律性”“消化”等词汇，而美国人则使用“食物组平衡”“避免脂肪和糖”等词汇，反映出不同的营养文化模型。社会文化语言学现象（SLP）的传统计算研究在自然语言处理中多通过特定群体或主题的定制化分析进行，需专门数据收集与实验操作化——此过程不适用于快速假设探索与原型开发。为此，我们提出构建一个“沙盒”，用于系统化、灵活的社会语言学研究。通过该方法，我们构建了一个按人口统计学/主题划分的Reddit数据集Splits!，经自我认同验证，并复现了现有文献中的若干已知SLP。我们通过可扩展的两阶段流程展示该沙盒的实用性：先从大规模“潜在SLP”（PSLPs）中筛选，再挖掘最具潜力的候选对象，以供深入定性研究。
Summary / 总结
Variation in language use, shaped by speakers' sociocultural background and specific context of use, offers a rich lens into cultural perspectives, values, and opinions.
为解决传统社会语言学研究需定制化数据收集与实验设计、难以快速探索假设的问题，作者提出Splits!这一灵活的计算社会语言学研究沙盒。该方法通过构建按人口统计与主题划分的Reddit语料库，结合自我标识与文献验证，实现对社会文化语言现象（SLP）的系统性挖掘。实验表明，其两阶段筛选流程能高效识别出具有文化差异的语言模式（如中西方营养话语差异），并支持快速原型验证与深入定性研究。
What do Language Models Learn and When? The Implicit Curriculum Hypothesis
Authors: Emmy Liu, Kaiser Sun, Millicent Li, Isabelle Lee, Lindia Tjuatja, Jen-tse Huang, Graham Neubig
First: 2026-04-09T17:50:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T17:50:12+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) can perform remarkably complex tasks, yet the fine-grained details of how these capabilities emerge during pretraining remain poorly understood. Scaling laws on validation loss tell us how much a model improves with additional compute, but not what skills it acquires in which order. To remedy this, we propose the Implicit Curriculum Hypothesis: pretraining follows a compositional and predictable curriculum across models and data mixtures. We test this by designing a suite of simple, composable tasks spanning retrieval, morphological transformations, coreference, logical reasoning, and mathematics. Using these tasks, we track emergence points across four model families spanning sizes from 410M-13B parameters. We find that emergence orderings of when models reach fixed accuracy thresholds are strikingly consistent ($ρ= .81$ across 45 model pairs), and that composite tasks most often emerge after their component tasks. Furthermore, we find that this structure is encoded in model representations: tasks with similar function vector representations also tend to follow similar trajectories in training. By using the space of representations derived from our task set, we can effectively predict the training trajectories of simple held-out compositional tasks throughout the course of pretraining ($R^2 = .68$-$.84$ across models) without previously evaluating them. Together, these results suggest that pretraining is more structured than loss curves reveal: skills emerge in a compositional order that is consistent across models and readable from their internals.
中文标题/摘要
标题：语言模型学习了什么以及何时学习？隐式课程假说
大型语言模型（LLMs）能够执行复杂任务，但其在预训练过程中能力如何逐步形成仍不甚明了。缩放定律告诉我们模型在增加计算量时验证损失的改善程度，却无法说明模型获得技能的顺序。为解决此问题，我们提出隐式课程假说：预训练在不同模型和数据混合中遵循可组合且可预测的课程。我们通过设计涵盖检索、形态变换、指代消解、逻辑推理和数学的简单可组合任务进行验证。利用这些任务，我们追踪了四个参数规模从4.1亿至130亿的模型家族中能力涌现点。我们发现，模型达到固定准确率阈值的涌现顺序高度一致（45对模型间相关系数ρ=0.81），且复合任务通常在其组成任务之后涌现。此外，我们发现这种结构编码在模型表示中：功能向量表示相似的任务往往在训练中遵循相似轨迹。通过使用从任务集中导出的表示空间，我们无需预先评估即可有效预测简单保留的组合任务在预训练全过程中的训练轨迹（模型间R²=0.68-0.84）。这些结果共同表明，预训练比损失曲线揭示的结构更有序：技能以可组合顺序涌现，且在不同模型间一致，可从模型内部读取。
Summary / 总结
Large language models (LLMs) can perform remarkably complex tasks, yet the fine-grained details of how these capabilities emerge during pretraining remain poorly understood.
该论文通过提出‘隐式课程假设’，探讨大语言模型在预训练中如何按组合顺序习得复杂能力，认为不同模型和数据混合下技能习得具有可预测的结构。作者设计了涵盖检索、形态变换、指代、推理和数学等任务的简单可组合任务集，追踪四类模型（410M–13B参数）达到固定准确率阈值的时间点。结果发现模型间技能涌现顺序高度一致（ρ=0.81），复合任务多在子任务之后出现；同时，功能向量相似的任务训练轨迹也相近，且基于这些表示可准确预测未见过的组合任务的训练曲线（R²=0.68–0.84），表明预训练过程存在内部结构化的隐式课程。
Visually-grounded Humanoid Agents
Authors: Hang Ye, Xiaoxuan Ma, Fan Lu, Wayne Wu, Kwan-Yee Lin, Yizhou Wang
First: 2026-04-09T17:50:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T17:50:09+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://alvinyh.github.io/VGHuman/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Digital human generation has been studied for decades and supports a wide range of real-world applications. However, most existing systems are passively animated, relying on privileged state or scripted control, which limits scalability to novel environments. We instead ask: how can digital humans actively behave using only visual observations and specified goals in novel scenes? Achieving this would enable populating any 3D environments with digital humans at scale that exhibit spontaneous, natural, goal-directed behaviors. To this end, we introduce Visually-grounded Humanoid Agents, a coupled two-layer (world-agent) paradigm that replicates humans at multiple levels: they look, perceive, reason, and behave like real people in real-world 3D scenes. The World Layer reconstructs semantically rich 3D Gaussian scenes from real-world videos via an occlusion-aware pipeline and accommodates animatable Gaussian-based human avatars. The Agent Layer transforms these avatars into autonomous humanoid agents, equipping them with first-person RGB-D perception and enabling them to perform accurate, embodied planning with spatial awareness and iterative reasoning, which is then executed at the low level as full-body actions to drive their behaviors in the scene. We further introduce a benchmark to evaluate humanoid-scene interaction in diverse reconstructed environments. Experiments show our agents achieve robust autonomous behavior, yielding higher task success rates and fewer collisions than ablations and state-of-the-art planning methods. This work enables active digital human population and advances human-centric embodied AI. Data, code, and models will be open-sourced.
中文标题/摘要
标题：视觉引导的人形代理
数字人生成已研究数十年，支持广泛的实际应用。然而，现有系统多为被动动画，依赖特权状态或脚本控制，限制了其在新环境中的可扩展性。我们提出的问题是：如何仅通过视觉观测和指定目标，使数字人在新场景中主动行为？实现这一目标将使数字人能够大规模地在任意3D环境中展现自发、自然、目标导向的行为。为此，我们引入了视觉引导的人形代理，这是一种耦合的双层（世界-代理）范式，从多个层面模拟人类：它们在真实3D场景中像真人一样观察、感知、推理和行动。世界层通过遮挡感知管道从真实视频重建语义丰富的3D高斯场景，并支持可动画的高斯人形化身。代理层将这些化身转化为自主人形代理，赋予其第一人称RGB-D感知能力，使其能进行准确的具身规划，具备空间感知和迭代推理能力，最终在低层以全身动作执行，驱动其在场景中的行为。我们进一步引入基准测试，用于评估人形与场景交互在多样化重建环境中的表现。实验表明，我们的代理实现了稳健的自主行为，任务成功率更高，碰撞更少，优于消融实验和现有最先进的规划方法。本工作实现了主动数字人部署，推动了以人为中心的具身AI发展。数据、代码和模型将开源。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for scalable, autonomous digital humans that can behave naturally in novel 3D environments without scripted control, this work introduces Visually-grounded Humanoid Agents, a two-layer system combining a World Layer that reconstructs semantically rich 3D scenes from videos and an Agent Layer that transforms animatable avatars into autonomous agents with first-person perception and embodied planning. The agents reason spatially and iteratively to execute full-body actions, enabling goal-directed behavior in real-world scenes. Experiments on a new benchmark demonstrate that these agents achieve higher task success rates and fewer collisions compared to ablated versions and state-of-the-art planning methods, validating their robustness and scalability.
为实现能在新环境中自主、自然交互的可扩展数字人类，该研究提出视觉引导的人形代理系统，包含重建语义丰富3D场景的世界层与赋予高斯模型人形自主感知与具身规划能力的代理层。代理通过第一人称RGB-D感知与空间推理执行全身动作，实现自发、目标导向的行为。实验表明，该系统在新基准测试中任务成功率更高、碰撞更少，优于消融实验与现有规划方法，验证了其在多样化虚拟环境中的鲁棒性与可扩展性。
Sumo: Dynamic and Generalizable Whole-Body Loco-Manipulation
Authors: John Z. Zhang, Maks Sorokin, Jan Brüdigam, Brandon Hung, Stephen Phillips, Dmitry Yershov, Farzad Niroui, Tong Zhao, Leonor Fermoselle, Xinghao Zhu, Chao Cao, Duy Ta, Tao Pang, Jiuguang Wang, Preston Culbertson, Zachary Manchester, Simon Le Cléac'h
First: 2026-04-09T17:49:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T17:49:40+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper presents a sim-to-real approach that enables legged robots to dynamically manipulate large and heavy objects with whole-body dexterity. Our key insight is that by performing test-time steering of a pre-trained whole-body control policy with a sample-based planner, we can enable these robots to solve a variety of dynamic loco-manipulation tasks. Interestingly, we find our method generalizes to a diverse set of objects and tasks with no additional tuning or training, and can be further enhanced by flexibly adjusting the cost function at test time. We demonstrate the capabilities of our approach through a variety of challenging loco-manipulation tasks on a Spot quadruped robot in the real world, including uprighting a tire heavier than the robot's nominal lifting capacity and dragging a crowd-control barrier larger and taller than the robot itself. Additionally, we show that the same approach can be generalized to humanoid loco-manipulation tasks, such as opening a door and pushing a table, in simulation. Project code and videos are available at \href{https://sumo.rai-inst.com/}{https://sumo.rai-inst.com/}.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Sumo：动态且可泛化的全身运动与操作
本文提出了一种仿真到现实的方案，使腿式机器人能够以全身灵巧性动态操作大型重物。我们的核心见解是：通过在测试时使用基于采样的规划器对预训练的全身控制策略进行转向，可使这些机器人解决多种动态运动与操作任务。有趣的是，我们的方法无需额外调参或训练，即可泛化至多样化的物体与任务，并可通过灵活调整测试时的成本函数进一步增强。我们在Spot四足机器人上通过多种具有挑战性的运动与操作任务验证了该方法的能力，包括扶正一个比机器人额定举升能力更重的轮胎，以及拖拽一个比机器人自身更大更高的防暴屏障。此外，我们还展示了该方法可泛化至人形机器人的运动与操作任务，如开门和推桌子，仿真结果亦已验证。项目代码与视频可在 \href{https://sumo.rai-inst.com/}{https://sumo.rai-inst.com/} 获取。

This paper presents a sim-to-real approach that enables legged robots to dynamically manipulate large and heavy objects with whole-body dexterity.
为使腿式机器人能够动态操控大型重物并展现全身灵巧性，本文提出一种从仿真到现实的方案，通过在测试时对预训练的全身控制策略进行样本规划引导。该方法无需额外调参或再训练，即可应对多样化的动态操控任务，且可通过调整测试时的成本函数进一步优化性能。实验表明，Spot四足机器人成功完成如举起超重轮胎、拖拽比自身更大的路障等高难度任务，且该方法可推广至仿真中的人形机器人任务，如开门和推桌子。
Differentially Private Language Generation and Identification in the Limit
Authors: Anay Mehrotra, Grigoris Velegkas, Xifan Yu, Felix Zhou
First: 2026-04-09T17:48:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T17:48:46+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We initiate the study of language generation in the limit, a model recently introduced by Kleinberg and Mullainathan [KM24], under the constraint of differential privacy. We consider the continual release model, where a generator must eventually output a stream of valid strings while protecting the privacy of the entire input sequence. Our first main result is that for countable collections of languages, privacy comes at no qualitative cost: we provide an $\varepsilon$-differentially-private algorithm that generates in the limit from any countable collection. This stands in contrast to many learning settings where privacy renders learnability impossible. However, privacy does impose a quantitative cost: there are finite collections of size $k$ for which uniform private generation requires $Ω(k/\varepsilon)$ samples, whereas just one sample suffices non-privately. We then turn to the harder problem of language identification in the limit. Here, we show that privacy creates fundamental barriers. We prove that no $\varepsilon$-DP algorithm can identify a collection containing two languages with an infinite intersection and a finite set difference, a condition far stronger than the classical non-private characterization of identification. Next, we turn to the stochastic setting where the sample strings are sampled i.i.d. from a distribution (instead of being generated by an adversary). Here, we show that private identification is possible if and only if the collection is identifiable in the adversarial model. Together, our results establish new dimensions along which generation and identification differ and, for identification, a separation between adversarial and stochastic settings induced by privacy constraints.
中文标题/摘要
标题：极限下的差分隐私语言生成与识别
我们首次在差分隐私约束下研究Kleinberg和Mullainathan [KM24]最近提出的极限语言生成模型。我们考虑持续发布模型，其中生成器必须最终输出有效字符串流，同时保护整个输入序列的隐私。我们的第一个主要结果是：对于可数语言集合，隐私不会带来定性代价——我们提供了一个ε-差分隐私算法，可从任意可数集合中极限生成。这与许多学习场景形成对比，在那些场景中隐私会使可学习性变得不可能。然而，隐私确实带来定量代价：对于大小为k的有限集合，均匀私有生成需要Ω(k/ε)个样本，而非私有情况下仅需一个样本。 随后我们转向更困难的极限语言识别问题。在此，我们证明隐私会带来根本性障碍：我们证明不存在ε-DP算法能识别包含两个无限交集但有限差集的语言集合，这一条件远强于经典非隐私识别的刻画。接着，我们转向随机设置，其中样本字符串独立同分布地从某个分布中采样（而非由对手生成）。在此，我们证明私有识别成立当且仅当该集合在对抗模型下可识别。综上，我们的结果揭示了生成与识别在新维度上的差异，并为识别问题建立了由隐私约束引发的对抗与随机设置之间的分离。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates differentially private language generation and identification in the limit, motivated by the need to understand how privacy constraints affect learning under infinite streams of data. The authors introduce an $\varepsilon$-differentially-private algorithm that can generate any language from a countable collection in the limit without qualitative loss, contrasting with other learning settings where privacy often blocks learnability. However, privacy imposes a quantitative cost: generating from finite collections requires $Ω(k/\varepsilon)$ samples, versus just one non-privately. For identification, privacy creates fundamental barriers—no $\varepsilon$-DP algorithm can identify collections with two languages having infinite intersection and finite difference, a condition stronger than the classical non-private requirement. In the stochastic setting, private identification is possible if and only if the collection is identifiable under adversarial sampling, revealing a privacy-induced separation between adversarial and stochastic models.
本文研究在差分隐私约束下语言生成与识别在极限情况下的可行性，旨在理解隐私对无限时延学习的影响。作者提出在可数语言集合上生成有效字符串的算法，证明隐私不带来定性障碍，但会带来量化代价：对大小为k的有限集合，私有生成需Ω(k/ε)个样本，而非私有只需一个样本。在识别方面，证明隐私存在根本限制：无ε-DP算法能识别包含两个无限交集且差集有限的语言集合，该条件比经典非隐私识别条件更强。在随机采样模型中（样本独立同分布），私有识别成立当且仅当集合在对抗性模型下可识别，揭示了隐私对对抗性与随机性模型间识别能力的分离作用。
Phantom: Physics-Infused Video Generation via Joint Modeling of Visual and Latent Physical Dynamics
Authors: Ying Shen, Jerry Xiong, Tianjiao Yu, Ismini Lourentzou
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-04-09T17:48:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T17:48:46+00:00
Comments: 15 pages, 6 figures, CVPR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent advances in generative video modeling, driven by large-scale datasets and powerful architectures, have yielded remarkable visual realism. However, emerging evidence suggests that simply scaling data and model size does not endow these systems with an understanding of the underlying physical laws that govern real-world dynamics. Existing approaches often fail to capture or enforce such physical consistency, resulting in unrealistic motion and dynamics. In his work, we investigate whether integrating the inference of latent physical properties directly into the video generation process can equip models with the ability to produce physically plausible videos. To this end, we propose Phantom, a Physics-Infused Video Generation model that jointly models the visual content and latent physical dynamics. Conditioned on observed video frames and inferred physical states, Phantom jointly predicts latent physical dynamics and generates future video frames. Phantom leverages a physics-aware video representation that serves as an abstract yet informaive embedding of the underlying physics, facilitating the joint prediction of physical dynamics alongside video content without requiring an explicit specification of a complex set of physical dynamics and properties. By integrating the inference of physical-aware video representation directly into the video generation process, Phantom produces video sequences that are both visually realistic and physically consistent. Quantitative and qualitative results on both standard video generation and physics-aware benchmarks demonstrate that Phantom not only outperforms existing methods in terms of adherence to physical dynamics but also delivers competitive perceptual fidelity.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Phantom：通过联合建模视觉与潜在物理动态实现物理增强的视频生成
近年来，得益于大规模数据集和强大架构的推动，生成式视频建模取得了显著的视觉真实感。然而，新证据表明，仅扩大数据和模型规模并不能赋予这些系统对支配现实世界动态的物理定律的理解。现有方法往往无法捕捉或强制执行物理一致性，导致运动和动态不真实。在本研究中，我们探讨将潜在物理属性的推断直接集成到视频生成过程中，是否能使模型生成物理上合理的视频。为此，我们提出了Phantom，一种物理增强的视频生成模型，联合建模视觉内容与潜在物理动态。在观察到的视频帧和推断的物理状态条件下，Phantom联合预测潜在物理动态并生成未来视频帧。Phantom利用一种物理感知的视频表示，作为底层物理的抽象但信息丰富的嵌入，从而无需显式指定复杂的物理动态和属性，即可联合预测物理动态与视频内容。通过将物理感知视频表示的推断直接集成到视频生成过程中，Phantom生成的视频序列既视觉真实又物理一致。在标准视频生成和物理感知基准测试上的定量与定性结果表明，Phantom不仅在遵循物理动态方面优于现有方法，还实现了具有竞争力的感知保真度。
Summary / 总结
Recent advances in generative video modeling, driven by large-scale datasets and powerful architectures, have yielded remarkable visual realism.
鉴于单纯扩大数据和模型规模无法赋予视频生成模型对物理规律的理解，作者提出Phantom模型，通过联合建模视觉内容与潜在物理动态来生成物理一致的视频。该模型引入一种物理感知的视频表示，抽象编码底层物理属性，使模型在生成未来帧的同时推断物理状态。实验表明，Phantom在物理合理性上优于现有方法，同时保持了良好的视觉真实感。
WebArbiter: A Principle-Guided Reasoning Process Reward Model for Web Agents
Authors: Yao Zhang, Shijie Tang, Zeyu Li, Zhen Han, Volker Tresp
Venue: ICLR 2026
First: 2026-01-29T15:39:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T17:48:11+00:00
Comments: Published as a conference paper at ICLR 2026. Extended version with additional experiments
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Web agents hold great potential for automating complex computer tasks, yet their interactions involve long-horizon, sequential decision-making with irreversible actions. In such settings, outcome-based supervision is sparse and delayed, often rewarding incorrect trajectories and failing to support inference-time scaling. This motivates the use of Process Reward Models (WebPRMs) for web navigation, but existing approaches remain limited: scalar WebPRMs collapse progress into coarse, weakly grounded signals, while checklist-based WebPRMs rely on brittle template matching that fails under layout or semantic changes and often mislabels superficially correct actions as successful, providing little insight or interpretability. To address these challenges, we introduce WebArbiter, a reasoning-first, principle-inducing WebPRM that formulates reward modeling as text generation, producing structured justifications that conclude with a preference verdict and identify the action most conducive to task completion under the current context. Training follows a two-stage pipeline: reasoning distillation equips the model with coherent principle-guided reasoning, and reinforcement learning corrects teacher biases by directly aligning verdicts with correctness, enabling stronger generalization. To support systematic evaluation, we release WebPRMBench, a comprehensive benchmark spanning four diverse web environments with rich tasks and high-quality preference annotations. On WebPRMBench, WebArbiter-7B outperforms the strongest baseline, GPT-5, by 9.1 points. In reward-guided trajectory search on WebArena-Lite, it surpasses the best prior WebPRM by up to 6.4 points, underscoring its robustness and practical value in complex web tasks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：WebArbiter：一种面向网络代理的基于原则引导的推理过程奖励模型
网络代理在自动化复杂计算机任务方面潜力巨大，但其交互涉及长时序、顺序决策与不可逆操作。此类场景下，基于结果的监督稀疏且延迟，常奖励错误轨迹，难以支持推理时扩展。这促使采用过程奖励模型（WebPRM）进行网络导航，但现有方法仍受限：标量WebPRM将进展压缩为粗略、弱关联信号；基于清单的WebPRM依赖脆弱模板匹配，易受布局或语义变化影响，常误将表面正确动作标记为成功，缺乏洞察力与可解释性。为应对这些挑战，我们提出WebArbiter，一种以推理优先、诱导原则的WebPRM，将奖励建模视为文本生成，输出结构化论证，最终给出偏好判决并识别当前情境下最有利于任务完成的动作。训练采用两阶段流程：推理蒸馏赋予模型连贯的原则引导推理能力；强化学习通过直接对齐判决与正确性修正教师偏差，实现更强泛化能力。为支持系统评估，我们发布WebPRMBench，涵盖四个多样网络环境、丰富任务及高质量偏好标注。在WebPRMBench上，WebArbiter-7B优于最强基线GPT-5达9.1分；在WebArena-Lite的奖励引导轨迹搜索中，其超越最佳前序WebPRM最高达6.4分，凸显其在复杂网络任务中的鲁棒性与实用价值。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for reliable, interpretable reward signals in long-horizon web agent navigation where outcome supervision is sparse and delayed, WebArbiter introduces a principle-guided reasoning process reward model that generates structured justifications ending in a preference verdict. It trains via a two-stage pipeline: first, reasoning distillation instills coherent, principle-based reasoning; second, reinforcement learning aligns verdicts with correctness to correct teacher biases. Evaluated on WebPRMBench, a new benchmark with diverse web environments and preference annotations, WebArbiter-7B outperforms the strongest baseline GPT-5 by 9.1 points and surpasses prior WebPRMs by up to 6.4 points on reward-guided trajectory search, demonstrating robust generalization and practical effectiveness.
为解决网络代理导航中反馈稀疏、延迟且不可逆导致的奖励建模难题，WebArbiter提出一种基于原则引导的推理过程奖励模型，通过生成结构化推理说明来评估动作。其训练采用两阶段流程：先通过推理蒸馏嵌入连贯原则，再通过强化学习对教师偏见进行修正，使判断与正确性对齐。在WebPRMBench基准测试中，WebArbiter-7B超越GPT-5 9.1分，在奖励引导轨迹搜索中较最优前序模型高6.4分，展现出更强的泛化能力和实际应用价值。
Quantifying Explanation Consistency: The C-Score Metric for CAM-Based Explainability in Medical Image Classification
Authors: Kabilan Elangovan, Daniel Ting
First: 2026-04-09T17:47:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T17:47:31+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Class Activation Mapping (CAM) methods are widely used to generate visual explanations for deep learning classifiers in medical imaging. However, existing evaluation frameworks assess whether explanations are correct, measured by localisation fidelity against radiologist annotations, rather than whether they are consistent: whether the model applies the same spatial reasoning strategy across different patients with the same pathology. We propose the C-Score (Consistency Score), a confidence-weighted, annotation-free metric that quantifies intra-class explanation reproducibility via intensity-emphasised pairwise soft IoU across correctly classified instances. We evaluate six CAM techniques: GradCAM, GradCAM++, LayerCAM, EigenCAM, ScoreCAM, and MS GradCAM++ across three CNN architectures (DenseNet201, InceptionV3, ResNet50V2) over thirty training epochs on the Kermany chest X-ray dataset, covering transfer learning and fine-tuning phases. We identify three distinct mechanisms of AUC-consistency dissociation, invisible to standard classification metrics: threshold-mediated gold list collapse, technique-specific attribution collapse at peak AUC, and class-level consistency masking in global aggregation. C-Score provides an early warning signal of impending model instability. ScoreCAM deterioration on ResNet50V2 is detectable one full checkpoint before catastrophic AUC collapse and yields architecture-specific clinical deployment recommendations grounded in explanation quality rather than predictive ranking alone.
中文标题/摘要
标题：量化解释一致性：用于医学图像分类的基于CAM的可解释性C-Score度量
类激活映射（CAM）方法广泛用于生成深度学习分类器在医学影像中的可视化解释。然而，现有评估框架评估的是解释是否正确，即通过定位保真度与放射科医生标注的对比，而非解释是否一致：即模型是否对具有相同病理的患者采用相同的空域推理策略。我们提出C-Score（一致性得分），这是一种基于置信度加权、无需标注的度量，通过在正确分类实例间进行强度强化的软IoU成对比较，量化同类解释的可重复性。我们在Kermany胸部X光数据集上，对六种CAM技术（GradCAM、GradCAM++、LayerCAM、EigenCAM、ScoreCAM、MS GradCAM++）在三种CNN架构（DenseNet201、InceptionV3、ResNet50V2）上进行评估，覆盖三十个训练周期，涵盖迁移学习与微调阶段。我们识别出三种标准分类指标无法察觉的AUC一致性解耦机制：阈值介导的黄金列表崩溃、技术特异性归因在AUC峰值时的崩溃，以及全局聚合中类级一致性被掩盖。C-Score可作为模型即将不稳定时的早期预警信号。在ResNet50V2架构上，ScoreCAM性能下降可在AUC崩溃前一个完整检查点被检测到，并据此提供基于解释质量而非仅预测排序的架构特定临床部署建议。
Summary / 总结
Class Activation Mapping (CAM) methods are widely used to generate visual explanations for deep learning classifiers in medical imaging.
为评估医学图像分类中CAM解释的一致性而非仅定位准确性，作者提出C-Score，一种基于置信度加权、无需标注的指标，通过强度强调的成对软IoU衡量同类样本解释的可重复性。在Kermany胸部X光数据集上对六种CAM方法和三种CNN架构进行30轮评估，C-Score揭示了三种标准分类指标无法捕捉的AUC-一致性分离机制。值得注意的是，它可作为模型不稳定性的早期预警，能在ResNet50V2架构下提前一个检查点发现ScoreCAM性能衰退，从而提供基于解释质量而非预测排名的临床部署建议。
sciwrite-lint: Verification Infrastructure for the Age of Science Vibe-Writing
Authors: Sergey V Samsonau
First: 2026-04-09T17:46:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T17:46:44+00:00
Comments: Code: https://github.com/authentic-research-partners/sciwrite-lint
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Science currently offers two options for quality assurance, both inadequate. Journal gatekeeping claims to verify both integrity and contribution, but actually measures prestige: peer review is slow, biased, and misses fabricated citations even at top venues. Open science provides no quality assurance at all: the only filter between AI-generated text and the public record is the author's integrity. AI-assisted writing makes both worse by producing more papers faster than either system can absorb. We propose a third option: measure the paper itself. sciwrite-lint (pip install sciwrite-lint) is an open-source linter for scientific manuscripts that runs entirely on the researcher's machine (free public databases, a single consumer GPU, and open-weights models) with no manuscripts sent to external services. The pipeline verifies that references exist, checks retraction status, compares metadata against canonical records, downloads and parses cited papers, verifies that they support the claims made about them, and follows one level further to check cited papers' own bibliographies. Each reference receives a per-reference reliability score aggregating all verification signals. We evaluate the pipeline on 30 unseen papers from arXiv and bioRxiv with error injection and LLM-adjudicated false positive analysis. As an experimental extension, we propose SciLint Score, combining integrity verification with a contribution component that operationalizes five frameworks from philosophy of science (Popper, Lakatos, Kitcher, Laudan, Mayo) into computable structural properties of scientific arguments. The integrity component is the core of the tool and is evaluated in this paper; the contribution component is released as experimental code for community development.
中文标题/摘要
标题：sciwrite-lint：科学氛围写作时代的验证基础设施
当前科学界提供两种质量保障方式，均不充分。期刊审稿声称验证论文的完整性和贡献度，实则衡量的是声望：同行评审缓慢、有偏见，甚至在顶级期刊中也会遗漏伪造引用。开放科学则完全无质量保障：AI生成文本与公共记录之间唯一的过滤器是作者的诚信。AI辅助写作使二者更糟，因为其能更快产出更多论文，而现有系统无法吸收。 我们提出第三种方案：直接衡量论文本身。sciwrite-lint（pip install sciwrite-lint）是一个开源的科学论文检查工具，完全在研究人员本地机器上运行（免费公开数据库、单张消费级GPU和开源模型权重），无需将论文发送至外部服务。该流程验证参考文献是否存在、检查撤稿状态、比对元数据与权威记录、下载并解析被引论文、验证其是否支持文中所述主张，并进一步追溯被引论文自身的参考文献。每条参考文献获得一个综合所有验证信号的可靠性评分。我们在arXiv和bioRxiv的30篇未见论文上评估该流程，采用错误注入和大语言模型仲裁的假阳性分析。 作为实验性扩展，我们提出SciLint评分，结合完整性验证与贡献度评估，将科学哲学中的五个框架（波普尔、拉卡托斯、基切尔、劳丹、梅奥）转化为可计算的科学论证结构属性。完整性部分是工具核心，本文中已评估；贡献部分作为实验性代码发布，供社区开发。
Summary / 总结
Science currently offers two options for quality assurance, both inadequate.
鉴于当前科学质量保障体系的不足——期刊同行评审重声望轻实质，开放科学则完全无验证机制——本研究提出sciwrite-lint，一个可在研究人员本地机器上运行的开源校验工具，利用公开数据库与开源模型自动验证论文。它检查文献引用是否存在、是否被撤回、元数据是否准确、引用是否支持论点，并递归验证被引文献的参考文献，为每条引用生成可靠性评分。在30篇未见论文上进行错误注入与大模型辅助的误报分析后，该工具展现出对伪造或不支持引用的高检出率，揭示了当前出版体系的系统性漏洞。
Constrained Policy Optimization with Cantelli-Bounded Value-at-Risk
Authors: Rohan Tangri, Jan-Peter Calliess
First: 2026-01-30T13:57:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T17:45:18+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce the Value-at-Risk Constrained Policy Optimization algorithm (VaR-CPO), a sample efficient and conservative method designed to optimize Value-at-Risk (VaR) constrained reinforcement learning (RL) problems. Empirically, we demonstrate that VaR-CPO is capable of safe exploration, achieving zero constraint violations during training in feasible environments, a critical property that baseline methods fail to uphold. To overcome the inherent non-differentiability of the VaR constraint, we employ Cantelli's inequality to obtain a tractable approximation based on the first two moments of the cost return. Additionally, by extending the trust-region framework of the Constrained Policy Optimization (CPO) method, we provide worst-case bounds for both policy improvement and constraint violation during the training process.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于Cantelli界值在风险价值约束下的策略优化
我们提出了价值在险约束策略优化算法（VaR-CPO），这是一种样本效率高且保守的方法，旨在优化受价值在险（VaR）约束的强化学习问题。实证表明，VaR-CPO能够实现安全探索，在可行环境中训练期间零约束违反，这是基线方法无法实现的关键特性。为克服VaR约束的固有不可微性，我们采用Cantelli不等式，基于成本回报的一阶和二阶矩获得可处理的近似解。此外，通过扩展约束策略优化（CPO）方法的信任域框架，我们为训练过程中策略改进和约束违反提供了最坏情况的界。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for safe and sample-efficient reinforcement learning under Value-at-Risk constraints, this paper proposes VaR-CPO, a method that uses Cantelli's inequality to approximate the non-differentiable VaR constraint via first- and second-moment statistics. By integrating this approximation into a trust-region framework, VaR-CPO ensures worst-case bounds on both policy improvement and constraint violation. Empirical results show that VaR-CPO achieves zero constraint violations during training in feasible environments, outperforming baseline methods that often violate constraints.
为实现受VaR约束下的安全且高效的强化学习，本文提出VaR-CPO算法，利用Cantelli不等式将不可微的VaR约束转化为基于一阶与二阶矩的可计算近似，并将其嵌入CPO的置信域框架中，以提供策略改进与约束违反的最坏情况界。实验表明，VaR-CPO在可行环境中训练期间实现零约束违反，优于常出现约束违规的基线方法。
Novel View Synthesis as Video Completion
Authors: Qi Wu, Khiem Vuong, Minsik Jeon, Srinivasa Narasimhan, Deva Ramanan
First: 2026-04-09T17:44:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T17:44:18+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://frame-crafter.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
We tackle the problem of sparse novel view synthesis (NVS) using video diffusion models; given $K$ ($\approx 5$) multi-view images of a scene and their camera poses, we predict the view from a target camera pose. Many prior approaches leverage generative image priors encoded via diffusion models. However, models trained on single images lack multi-view knowledge. We instead argue that video models already contain implicit multi-view knowledge and so should be easier to adapt for NVS. Our key insight is to formulate sparse NVS as a low frame-rate video completion task. However, one challenge is that sparse NVS is defined over an unordered set of inputs, often too sparse to admit a meaningful order, so the models should be $\textit{invariant}$ to permutations of that input set. To this end, we present FrameCrafter, which adapts video models (naturally trained with coherent frame orderings) to permutation-invariant NVS through several architectural modifications, including per-frame latent encodings and removal of temporal positional embeddings. Our results suggest that video models can be easily trained to "forget" about time with minimal supervision, producing competitive performance on sparse-view NVS benchmarks. Project page: https://frame-crafter.github.io/
中文标题/摘要
标题：将新颖视角合成视为视频补全
我们利用视频扩散模型解决稀疏新颖视角合成（NVS）问题：给定场景的K（约5）个多视角图像及其相机位姿，预测目标相机位姿下的视角。许多现有方法依赖通过扩散模型编码的生成图像先验。然而，仅在单张图像上训练的模型缺乏多视角知识。我们主张视频模型已隐含多视角知识，因此更适合用于NVS。我们的核心思路是将稀疏NVS建模为低帧率视频补全任务。但一个挑战是，稀疏NVS定义在无序输入集合上，通常过于稀疏而无法赋予有意义的顺序，因此模型应对此输入集合的排列具有不变性。为此，我们提出了FrameCrafter，通过若干架构修改（包括每帧潜在编码和移除时间位置嵌入），将天然训练于有序帧的视频模型适配为排列不变的NVS模型。实验结果表明，视频模型在极小监督下即可轻松“遗忘”时间信息，在稀疏视角NVS基准测试中表现优异。项目页面：https://frame-crafter.github.io/
Summary / 总结
We tackle the problem of sparse novel view synthesis (NVS) using video diffusion models; given $K$ ($\approx 5$) multi-view images of a scene and their camera poses, we predict the view from a target camera pose.
为了解决单图像扩散模型在稀疏新视角合成中缺乏多视角一致性的问题，本文将该任务视为视频补全问题，利用视频模型中固有的多视角隐含知识。方法FrameCrafter通过修改架构（如帧级潜在编码和移除时间位置嵌入）使视频模型对输入视角顺序不变。实验表明，在极少量监督下，该方法能有效训练视频模型忽略时间顺序，同时在稀疏视角合成基准上取得竞争力结果，证明视频模型可被适配用于多视角重建。
SealQA: Raising the Bar for Reasoning in Search-Augmented Language Models
Authors: Thinh Pham, Nguyen Nguyen, Pratibha Zunjare, Weiyuan Chen, Yu-Min Tseng, Tu Vu
Venue: ICLR 2026
First: 2025-06-01T16:04:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T17:44:11+00:00
Comments: Camera Ready version for ICLR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
We introduce SealQA, a new challenge benchmark for evaluating SEarch-Augmented Language models on fact-seeking questions where web search yields conflicting, noisy, or unhelpful results. SealQA comes in three flavors: (1) Seal-0 (main) and (2) Seal-Hard, which assess factual accuracy and reasoning capabilities, with Seal-0 focusing on the most challenging questions where chat models (e.g., GPT-4.1) typically achieve near-zero accuracy; and (3) LongSeal, which extends SealQA to test long-context, multi-document reasoning in "needle-in-a-haystack" settings. Our evaluation reveals critical limitations in current models: Even frontier LLMs perform poorly across all SealQA flavors. On Seal-0, frontier agentic models equipped with tools like o3 and o4-mini achieve only 17.1% and 6.3% accuracy, respectively, at their best reasoning efforts. We find that advanced reasoning models such as DeepSeek-R1-671B and o3-mini are highly vulnerable to noisy search results. Notably, increasing test-time compute does not yield reliable gains across o3-mini, o4-mini, and o3, with performance often plateauing or even declining early. Additionally, while recent models are less affected by the "lost-in-the-middle" issue, they still fail to reliably identify relevant documents in LongSeal when faced with numerous distractors. To facilitate future work, we release SealQA at huggingface.co/datasets/vtllms/sealqa.
Summary / 总结
SealQA was developed to evaluate how well search-augmented language models handle fact-seeking questions where web search returns conflicting, noisy, or unhelpful results. The benchmark includes three variants: Seal-0 (testing factual accuracy on hardest questions), Seal-Hard (assessing reasoning), and LongSeal (evaluating multi-document, long-context reasoning). Experiments show that even state-of-the-art models like o3 and o3-mini perform poorly, achieving only 17.1% and 6.3% accuracy on Seal-0, respectively. Advanced models such as DeepSeek-R1-671B and o3-mini are highly sensitive to noisy search results, and increasing test-time compute does not consistently improve performance. Models also struggle to identify relevant documents in LongSeal despite having access to many distractors.
为评估语言模型在现实搜索环境下（常返回冲突或噪声结果）的推理能力，作者提出了SealQA基准，包含三个变体：Seal-0（聚焦高难度事实题）、Seal-Hard（测试推理能力）和LongSeal（测试长上下文多文档推理）。实验表明，即使前沿模型如o3和o3-mini在Seal-0上最高也仅分别达到17.1%和6.3%的准确率，且对噪声搜索结果高度敏感。增加测试时计算量无法稳定提升性能，模型在LongSeal中面对大量干扰文档时仍难以准确识别相关文档。
The Detection-Extraction Gap: Models Know the Answer Before They Can Say It
Authors: Hanyang Wang, Mingxuan Zhu
First: 2026-04-08T02:47:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T17:43:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Modern reasoning models continue generating long after the answer is already determined. Across five model configurations, two families, and three benchmarks, we find that 52--88% of chain-of-thought tokens are produced after the answer is recoverable from a partial prefix. This post-commitment generation reveals a structural phenomenon: the detection-extraction gap. Free continuations from early prefixes recover the correct answer even at 10% of the trace, while forced extraction fails on 42% of these cases. The answer is recoverable from the model state, yet prompt-conditioned decoding fails to extract it. We formalize this mismatch via a total-variation bound between free and forced continuation distributions, yielding quantitative estimates of suffix-induced shift. Exploiting this asymmetry, we propose Black-box Adaptive Early Exit (BAEE), which uses free continuations for both detection and extraction, truncating 70--78% of serial generation while improving accuracy by 1--5pp across all models. For thinking-mode models, early exit prevents post-commitment overwriting, yielding gains of up to 5.8pp; a cost-optimized variant achieves 68--73% reduction at a median of 9 API calls. Code is available at https://github.com/EdWangLoDaSc/know2say.
中文标题/摘要
标题：检测-提取差距：模型在能说出答案前已知答案
现代推理模型在答案已确定后仍持续生成。在五种模型配置、两个家族和三个基准测试中，我们发现52%至88%的思维链令牌是在从部分前缀可恢复答案后生成的。这种后承诺生成揭示了一种结构性现象：检测-提取差距。早期前缀的自由延续即使在轨迹的10%处也能恢复正确答案，而强制提取在42%的情况下失败。答案可从模型状态中恢复，但提示条件下的解码无法提取它。我们通过自由延续与强制延续分布之间的总变差界形式化这一不匹配，从而量化后缀诱导的偏移。利用这一不对称性，我们提出黑盒自适应早期退出（BAEE），该方法利用自由延续进行检测和提取，截断70%至78%的串行生成，同时在所有模型上提升准确率1至5个百分点。对于思维模式模型，早期退出可防止后承诺覆盖，最高提升5.8个百分点；一个成本优化版本在中位数9次API调用下实现68%至73%的减少。代码见https://github.com/EdWangLoDaSc/know2say。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates why modern reasoning models generate unnecessary tokens after the answer is already determined, revealing a structural gap between detecting and extracting the correct answer. The authors analyze five model configurations across three benchmarks and find that 52–88% of chain-of-thought tokens are generated after the answer is recoverable from a partial prefix. They formalize this phenomenon using a total-variation bound between free and forced continuation distributions, showing that free continuations can recover the correct answer even at 10% of the trace, while forced extraction fails in 42% of cases. Based on this asymmetry, they propose Black-box Adaptive Early Exit (BAEE), which uses free continuations for both detection and extraction, achieving up to 78% truncation of generation while improving accuracy by 1–5pp across models. For thinking-mode models, early exit prevents post-commitment overwriting, boosting accuracy by up to 5.8pp, and a cost-optimized variant reduces API calls by 68–73% at a median of 9 calls.
该论文研究了现代推理模型在答案已确定后仍持续生成冗余内容的现象，揭示了“检测-提取间隙”这一结构性问题。作者在五个模型配置、三个基准测试中发现，52%至88%的思维链生成内容是在答案已可从部分前缀中恢复后才产生的。他们通过自由延续与强制延续分布之间的总变差界量化了这一差距，发现仅10%的生成轨迹中，自由延续即可正确恢复答案，而强制提取在42%的情况下失败。为此，他们提出黑盒自适应早期退出（BAEE）方法，利用自由延续进行检测与提取，实现高达78%的生成截断，同时在所有模型上提升准确率1–5个百分点。对于思维模式模型，早期退出可防止后续覆盖，最高提升准确率5.8个百分点；其成本优化版本在中位数9次API调用下，API调用减少68–73%。
BoBa: Boosting Backdoor Detection through Data Distribution Inference in Federated Learning
Authors: Zhengyuan Jiang, Xingyu Lyu, Shanghao Shi, Yang Xiao, Yimin Chen, Y. Thomas Hou, Wenjing Lou, Ning Wanga
Venue: ECAI 2025
First: 2024-07-12T19:38:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T17:42:49+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Federated learning, while being a promising approach for collaborative model training, is susceptible to backdoor attacks due to its decentralized nature. Backdoor attacks have shown remarkable stealthiness, as they compromise model predictions only when inputs contain specific triggers. As a countermeasure, anomaly detection is widely used to filter out backdoor attacks in FL. However, the non-independent and identically distributed (non-IID) data distribution nature of FL clients presents substantial challenges in backdoor attack detection, as the data variety introduces variance among benign models, making them indistinguishable from malicious ones. In this work, we propose a novel distribution-aware backdoor detection mechanism, BoBa, to address this problem. To differentiate outliers arising from data variety versus backdoor attacks, we propose to break down the problem into two steps: clustering clients utilizing their data distribution, and followed by a voting-based detection. We propose a novel data distribution inference mechanism for accurate data distribution estimation. To improve detection robustness, we introduce an overlapping clustering method, where each client is associated with multiple clusters, ensuring that the trustworthiness of a model update is assessed collectively by multiple clusters rather than a single cluster. Through extensive evaluations, we demonstrate that BoBa can reduce the attack success rate to lower than 0.001 while maintaining high main task accuracy across various attack strategies and experimental settings.
中文标题/摘要
标题：BoBa：通过数据分布推断提升联邦学习中的后门检测
联邦学习虽是协同模型训练的有前景方法，但因其去中心化特性易受后门攻击。后门攻击具有高度隐蔽性，仅在输入包含特定触发器时才会破坏模型预测。作为应对措施，异常检测常用于过滤联邦学习中的后门攻击。然而，联邦学习客户端数据分布的非独立同分布（non-IID）特性给后门检测带来重大挑战，因数据多样性导致良性模型间存在差异，难以与恶意模型区分。 本工作中，我们提出一种新颖的分布感知后门检测机制BoBa以解决此问题。为区分数据多样性引起的异常与后门攻击，我们将问题分解为两步：利用客户端数据分布进行聚类，随后进行投票式检测。我们提出一种新颖的数据分布推断机制以实现精确的数据分布估计。为提升检测鲁棒性，我们引入重叠聚类方法，每个客户端关联多个聚类，确保模型更新的可信度由多个聚类共同评估而非单一聚类。通过大量评估，我们证明BoBa可在多种攻击策略和实验设置下，将攻击成功率降至低于0.001，同时保持高主任务准确率。
Summary / 总结
Federated learning, while being a promising approach for collaborative model training, is susceptible to backdoor attacks due to its decentralized nature.
为应对联邦学习中因非独立同分布数据导致的后门攻击检测困难，本文提出BoBa方法，通过数据分布推断对客户端进行聚类，并采用投票机制区分恶意更新与良性更新，引入重叠聚类以提升检测鲁棒性。实验表明，BoBa可将攻击成功率降至0.001以下，同时在多种攻击场景下保持高主任务准确率。
PIArena: A Platform for Prompt Injection Evaluation
Authors: Runpeng Geng, Chenlong Yin, Yanting Wang, Ying Chen, Jinyuan Jia
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-04-09T17:42:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T17:42:45+00:00
Comments: To appear in ACL 2026. The code is available at https://github.com/sleeepeer/PIArena
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Prompt injection attacks pose serious security risks across a wide range of real-world applications. While receiving increasing attention, the community faces a critical gap: the lack of a unified platform for prompt injection evaluation. This makes it challenging to reliably compare defenses, understand their true robustness under diverse attacks, or assess how well they generalize across tasks and benchmarks. For instance, many defenses initially reported as effective were later found to exhibit limited robustness on diverse datasets and attacks. To bridge this gap, we introduce PIArena, a unified and extensible platform for prompt injection evaluation that enables users to easily integrate state-of-the-art attacks and defenses and evaluate them across a variety of existing and new benchmarks. We also design a dynamic strategy-based attack that adaptively optimizes injected prompts based on defense feedback. Through comprehensive evaluation using PIArena, we uncover critical limitations of state-of-the-art defenses: limited generalizability across tasks, vulnerability to adaptive attacks, and fundamental challenges when an injected task aligns with the target task. The code and datasets are available at https://github.com/sleeepeer/PIArena.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PIArena：提示注入评估平台
提示注入攻击对各类现实应用构成严重安全风险。尽管该问题日益受到关注，但社区仍面临关键缺口：缺乏统一的提示注入评估平台。这使得可靠比较防御措施、理解其在多样化攻击下的真实鲁棒性，或评估其在不同任务和基准上的泛化能力变得困难。例如，许多最初被报告为有效的防御措施，后来被发现对多样化数据集和攻击手段的鲁棒性有限。为弥合这一差距，我们提出了PIArena，一个统一且可扩展的提示注入评估平台，使用户能够轻松集成最先进的攻击与防御方法，并在多种现有及新基准上进行评估。我们还设计了一种基于动态策略的攻击方法，该方法能根据防御反馈自适应优化注入提示。通过使用PIArena进行全面评估，我们揭示了当前最先进防御措施的关键局限性：任务间泛化能力有限、易受自适应攻击影响，以及当注入任务与目标任务一致时所面临的根本性挑战。代码与数据集可在 https://github.com/sleeepeer/PIArena 获取。
Summary / 总结
Prompt injection attacks pose serious security risks across a wide range of real-world applications.
为解决当前缺乏统一评估平台导致的防御方法比较困难的问题，作者提出了PIArena平台，支持攻击与防御方法在多种基准上的灵活集成，并设计了一种基于动态策略的攻击方法，能根据防御反馈自适应优化注入提示。实验结果表明，现有先进防御方法普遍存在任务泛化能力差、易受自适应攻击影响，以及在注入任务与目标任务一致时存在根本性缺陷，凸显了构建更可靠评估框架的必要性。
Bridging the Gap between Micro-scale Traffic Simulation and 4D Digital Cityscapes
Authors: Longxiang Jiao, Lukas Hofmann, Yiru Yang, Zhanyi Wu, Jonas Egeler
First: 2026-04-09T17:40:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T17:40:51+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
While micro-scale traffic simulations provide essential data for urban planning, they are rarely coupled with the high-fidelity visualization or auralization necessary for effective stakeholder communication. In this work, we present a real-time 4D visualization framework that couples the SUMO traffic with a photorealistic, geospatially accurate VR representation of Zurich in Unreal Engine 5. Our architecture implements a robust C++ data pipeline for synchronized vehicle visualization and features an Open Sound Control (OSC) interface to support external auralization engines. We validate the framework through a user study assessing the correlation between simulated traffic dynamics and human perception. Results demonstrate a high degree of perceptual alignment, where users correctly interpret safety risks from the 4D simulation. Furthermore, our findings indicate that the inclusion of spatialized audio alters the user's sense of safety, showing the importance of multimodality in traffic simulations.

标题：弥合微观交通仿真与四维数字城市景观之间的差距
