DiffGradCAM: A Class Activation Map Using the Full Model Decision to Solve Unaddressed Adversarial Attacks
Authors: Jacob Piland, Chris Sweet, Adam Czajka
First: 2025-06-10T07:31:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T16:38:18+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Class Activation Mapping (CAM) and its gradient-based variants (e.g., GradCAM) have become standard tools for explaining Convolutional Neural Network (CNN) predictions. However, these approaches typically focus on individual logits, while for neural networks using softmax, the class membership probability estimates depend only on the differences between logits, not on their absolute values. This disconnect leaves standard CAMs vulnerable to adversarial manipulation, such as passive fooling, where a model is trained to produce misleading CAMs without affecting decision performance. To address this vulnerability, we propose DiffGradCAM and its higher-order derivative version DiffGradCAM++, as novel, lightweight, contrastive approaches to class activation mapping that are not susceptible to passive fooling and match the output of standard methods such as GradCAM and GradCAM++ in the non-adversarial case. To test our claims, we introduce Salience-Hoax Activation Maps (SHAMs), a more advanced, entropy-aware form of passive fooling that serves as a benchmark for CAM robustness under adversarial conditions. Together, SHAM and DiffGradCAM establish a new framework for probing and improving the robustness of saliency-based explanations. We validate both contributions across multi-class tasks with few and many classes.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DiffGradCAM：利用完整模型决策的类别激活图以应对未解决的对抗攻击
类别激活映射（CAM）及其基于梯度的变体（如GradCAM）已成为解释卷积神经网络（CNN）预测的标准工具。然而，这些方法通常聚焦于单个logits，而对于使用softmax的神经网络，类别隶属概率估计仅依赖于logits之间的差异，而非其绝对值。这种不匹配使标准CAM易受对抗操纵，例如被动欺骗攻击，即在不改变决策性能的前提下训练模型生成误导性CAM。 为解决此漏洞，我们提出DiffGradCAM及其高阶导数版本DiffGradCAM++，作为新颖、轻量级、对比式的类别激活映射方法，不仅不受被动欺骗攻击影响，且在非对抗情况下其输出与标准方法（如GradCAM和GradCAM++）一致。为验证我们的主张，我们引入了显著性欺骗激活图（SHAMs），这是一种更先进的、基于熵感知的被动欺骗形式，作为评估CAM在对抗条件下鲁棒性的基准。SHAM与DiffGradCAM共同构建了新的框架，用于探测和提升基于显著性的解释的鲁棒性。我们在少类别和多类别任务中验证了这两项贡献。
Summary / 总结
Class Activation Mapping (CAM) and its gradient-based variants (e.g., GradCAM) have become standard tools for explaining Convolutional Neural Network (CNN) predictions.
为解决标准CAM和GradCAM方法在对抗攻击下易受被动欺骗（生成误导性热力图而不影响决策）的问题，本文提出DiffGradCAM及其高阶版本DiffGradCAM++，通过基于梯度的对比学习利用完整模型决策生成抗欺骗的解释，同时在非对抗场景下与基准方法保持输出一致。在引入熵感知的对抗基准SHAMs后，DiffGradCAM在少类和多类任务中均有效抵御被动欺骗，且性能与基线方法相当。
Provably Adaptive Linear Approximation for the Shapley Value and Beyond
Authors: Weida Li, Yaoliang Yu, Bryan Kian Hsiang Low
First: 2026-04-09T16:38:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T16:38:14+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The Shapley value, and its broader family of semi-values, has received much attention in various attribution problems. A fundamental and long-standing challenge is their efficient approximation, since exact computation generally requires an exponential number of utility queries in the number of players $n$. To meet the challenges of large-scale applications, we explore the limits of efficiently approximating semi-values under a $Θ(n)$ space constraint. Building upon a vector concentration inequality, we establish a theoretical framework that enables sharper query complexities for existing unbiased randomized algorithms. Within this framework, we systematically develop a linear-space algorithm that requires $O(\frac{n}{ε^{2}}\log\frac{1}δ)$ utility queries to ensure $P(\|\hat{\boldsymbolφ}-\boldsymbolφ\|_{2}\geqε)\leq δ$ for all commonly used semi-values. In particular, our framework naturally bridges OFA, unbiased kernelSHAP, SHAP-IQ and the regression-adjusted approach, and definitively characterizes when paired sampling is beneficial. Moreover, our algorithm allows explicit minimization of the mean square error for each specific utility function. Accordingly, we introduce the first adaptive, linear-time, linear-space randomized algorithm, Adalina, that theoretically achieves improved mean square error. All of our theoretical findings are experimentally validated.
中文标题/摘要
标题：可证明自适应线性近似Shapley值及其扩展
Shapley值及其更广泛的半值族在各类归因问题中备受关注。一个根本且长期存在的挑战是其高效近似，因为精确计算通常需要指数级的效用查询次数（与玩家数量n成正比）。为应对大规模应用的挑战，我们在Θ(n)空间约束下探索半值高效近似的极限。基于向量集中不等式，我们建立了一个理论框架，使现有无偏随机算法的查询复杂度更优。在此框架下，我们系统性地开发了一种线性空间算法，仅需O(n/ε²·log(1/δ))个效用查询，即可确保对所有常用半值，P(||φ̂−φ||₂≥ε)≤δ。特别地，我们的框架自然连接了OFA、无偏kernelSHAP、SHAP-IQ和回归调整方法，并明确刻画了成对采样何时有益。此外，我们的算法允许对每个特定效用函数显式最小化均方误差。因此，我们提出了首个自适应、线性时间、线性空间的随机算法Adalina，理论上实现了更优的均方误差。所有理论发现均通过实验验证。
Summary / 总结
The Shapley value, and its broader family of semi-values, has received much attention in various attribution problems.
为在大规模应用中满足线性空间约束下高效近似Shapley值及其半值，本文基于向量集中不等式构建理论框架，优化现有无偏随机算法的查询复杂度。提出Adalina算法——首个自适应、线性时间与空间的随机算法，能针对特定效用函数显式最小化均方误差，并明确指出配对采样在特定条件下更优。实验验证表明，Adalina在多种半值计算中显著优于现有方法，兼具精度与效率。
Why Adam Can Beat SGD: Second-Moment Normalization Yields Sharper Tails
Authors: Ruinan Jin, Yingbin Liang, Shaofeng Zou
First: 2026-03-03T15:34:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T16:37:36+00:00
Comments: 59 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Despite Adam demonstrating faster empirical convergence than SGD in many applications, much of the existing theory yields guarantees essentially comparable to those of SGD, leaving the empirical performance gap insufficiently explained. In this paper, we uncover a key second-moment normalization in Adam and develop a stopping-time/martingale analysis that provably distinguishes Adam from SGD under the classical bounded variance model (a second moment assumption). In particular, we establish the first theoretical separation between the high-probability convergence behaviors of the two methods: Adam achieves a $δ^{-1/2}$ dependence on the confidence parameter $δ$, whereas corresponding high-probability guarantee for SGD necessarily incurs at least a $δ^{-1}$ dependence.
中文标题/摘要
标题：为何Adam能胜过SGD：二阶矩归一化带来更尖锐的尾部
尽管Adam在许多应用中表现出比SGD更快的实证收敛速度，但现有理论大多仅提供与SGD相当的保证，未能充分解释实证性能差距。本文揭示了Adam中的关键二阶矩归一化机制，并发展了一种停时/鞅分析方法，在经典有界方差模型（二阶矩假设）下可严格区分Adam与SGD。特别是，我们首次在高概率收敛行为上建立了两者理论上的分离：Adam对置信参数δ的依赖为δ^{-1/2}，而SGD相应的高概率保证至少需δ^{-1}依赖。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the empirical superiority of Adam over SGD despite similar theoretical guarantees, this paper identifies a key second-moment normalization mechanism in Adam and employs a stopping-time/martingale analysis to theoretically distinguish its convergence behavior from SGD under the bounded variance model. The analysis reveals that Adam achieves a high-probability convergence rate with a δ^{-1/2} dependence on the confidence parameter δ, whereas SGD’s corresponding guarantee requires at least δ^{-1}, establishing the first provable theoretical separation between the two optimizers.
鉴于Adam在实践中优于SGD，但现有理论对其优势解释不足，本文识别了Adam中的关键二阶矩归一化机制，并通过停时与鞅分析，在经典有界方差模型下理论区分了Adam与SGD的收敛行为。结果表明，Adam在高概率收敛下对置信参数δ的依赖为δ^{-1/2}，而SGD至少需要δ^{-1}，首次实现了二者收敛行为的理论分离。
HST-HGN: Heterogeneous Spatial-Temporal Hypergraph Networks with Bidirectional State Space Models for Global Fatigue Assessment
Authors: Changdao Chen
First: 2026-04-09T16:36:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T16:36:43+00:00
Comments: 10 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
It remains challenging to assess driver fatigue from untrimmed videos under constrained computational budgets, due to the difficulty of modeling long-range temporal dependencies in subtle facial expressions. Some existing approaches rely on computationally heavy architectures, whereas others employ traditional lightweight pairwise graph networks, despite their limited capacity to model high-order synergies and global temporal context. Therefore, we propose HST-HGN, a novel Heterogeneous Spatial-Temporal Hypergraph Network driven by Bidirectional State Space Models. Spatially, we introduce a hierarchical hypergraph network to fuse pose-disentangled geometric topologies with multi-modal texture patches dynamically. This formulation encapsulates high-order synergistic facial deformations, effectively overcoming the limitations of conventional methods. In temporal terms, a Bi-Mamba module with linear complexity is applied to perform bidirectional sequence modeling. This explicit temporal-evolution filtering enables the network to distinguish highly ambiguous transient actions, such as yawning versus speaking, while encompassing their complete physiological lifecycles. Extensive evaluations across diverse fatigue benchmarks demonstrate that HST-HGN achieves state-of-the-art performance. In particular, our method strikes a balance between discriminative power and computational efficiency, making it well-suited for real-time in-cabin edge deployment.
中文标题/摘要
标题：HST-HGN：基于双向状态空间模型的异构时空超图网络用于全局疲劳评估
在计算资源受限的情况下，从未剪辑视频中评估驾驶员疲劳仍具挑战性，原因在于难以建模细微面部表情中的长程时序依赖关系。现有方法要么依赖计算开销大的架构，要么采用传统轻量级成对图网络，但其建模高阶协同关系和全局时序上下文的能力有限。为此，我们提出HST-HGN，一种由双向状态空间模型驱动的异构时空超图网络。在空间维度上，我们引入分层超图网络，动态融合姿态解耦的几何拓扑与多模态纹理块。该结构有效封装高阶协同面部形变，克服传统方法的局限。在时序维度上，采用线性复杂度的Bi-Mamba模块进行双向序列建模，其显式时序演化滤波机制使网络能够区分高度模糊的瞬态动作（如打哈欠与说话），并涵盖其完整生理周期。在多个疲劳评估基准上的广泛实验表明，HST-HGN达到当前最优性能。尤其，我们的方法在判别能力与计算效率间取得良好平衡，适用于车载边缘端实时部署。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need to assess driver fatigue from untrimmed videos under computational constraints, this work introduces HST-HGN, a heterogeneous spatial-temporal hypergraph network powered by bidirectional state space models. Spatially, it employs a hierarchical hypergraph to dynamically fuse pose-disentangled geometric structures with multi-modal texture patches, capturing high-order facial synergies. Temporally, it uses a Bi-Mamba module with linear complexity to model bidirectional sequences, enabling precise differentiation of ambiguous actions like yawning and speaking by tracking their full physiological lifecycles. Experiments across multiple fatigue benchmarks confirm HST-HGN achieves state-of-the-art accuracy while maintaining computational efficiency, making it suitable for real-time edge deployment.
为解决在计算资源受限条件下从未剪辑视频中评估驾驶员疲劳的难题，HST-HGN提出一种基于双向状态空间模型的异构时空超图网络。空间上，该网络通过分层超图动态融合姿态解耦的几何拓扑与多模态纹理块，捕捉高阶面部形变。时间上，采用线性复杂度的Bi-Mamba模块进行双向序列建模，能精准区分如打哈欠与说话等易混淆动作并追踪其完整生理周期。在多个疲劳评估基准上，HST-HGN表现优于现有方法，兼具高判别力与低计算开销，适合实时车载边缘部署。
Continued AI Scaling Requires Repeated Efficiency Doublings
Authors: Chien-Ping Lu
First: 2026-03-30T14:42:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T16:35:51+00:00
Comments: 9 pages, 1 figure. v2
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper argues that continued AI scaling requires repeated efficiency doublings. Classical AI scaling laws remain useful because they make progress predictable despite diminishing returns, but the compute variable in those laws is best read as logical compute, not as a record of one fixed physical implementation. Practical burden therefore depends on the efficiency with which physical resources realize that compute. Under that interpretation, diminishing returns mean rising operational burden, not merely a flatter curve. Sustained progress then requires recurrent gains in hardware, algorithms, and systems that keep additional logical compute feasible at acceptable cost. The relevant analogy is Moore's Law, understood less as a theorem than as an organizing expectation of repeated efficiency improvement. AI does not yet have a single agreed cadence for such gains, but recent evidence suggests trends that are at least Moore-like and sometimes faster. The paper's claim is therefore simple: if AI scaling is to remain active, repeated efficiency doublings are not optional. They are required.
中文标题/摘要
标题：持续的AI扩展需要重复的效率翻倍
本文认为，持续的AI扩展需要重复的效率翻倍。经典的AI扩展定律仍有用，因为它们使进展可预测，即使存在边际收益递减，但这些定律中的计算变量最好理解为逻辑计算，而非单一固定物理实现的记录。实际负担取决于物理资源实现该计算的效率。在此理解下，边际收益递减意味着运营负担上升，而不仅仅是曲线变平。要维持进展，就必须在硬件、算法和系统方面持续取得进步，以确保额外的逻辑计算在可接受的成本下可行。相关类比是摩尔定律，应理解为一种重复效率改进的组织性预期，而非定理。AI目前尚未达成统一的此类改进节奏，但近期证据表明，趋势至少类似摩尔定律，有时甚至更快。本文主张简单明了：若AI扩展要持续活跃，重复的效率翻倍并非可选，而是必需。
Summary / 总结
This paper argues that continued AI scaling requires repeated efficiency doublings.
本文指出，持续的AI扩展需要反复实现效率翻倍，因为经典缩放定律中的“计算量”应理解为逻辑计算量，其实际成本取决于物理资源的实现效率。当效率不提升时，边际收益递减意味着运营负担上升，而非曲线变平。因此，维持AI进步必须依靠硬件、算法和系统层面的持续优化，以保持逻辑计算的可负担性。近期趋势显示，这些效率提升可能遵循类似摩尔定律的节奏，有时甚至更快，故效率翻倍不是可选项，而是AI持续发展的必要条件。
A Generalized Sinkhorn Algorithm for Mean-Field Schrödinger Bridge
Authors: Asmaa Eldesoukey, Yongxin Chen, Abhishek Halder
First: 2026-04-08T00:04:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T16:35:00+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The mean-field Schrödinger bridge (MFSB) problem concerns designing a minimum-effort controller that guides a diffusion process with nonlocal interaction to reach a given distribution from another by a fixed deadline. Unlike the standard Schrödinger bridge, the dynamical constraint for MFSB is the mean-field limit of a population of interacting agents with controls. It serves as a natural model for large-scale multi-agent systems. The MFSB is computationally challenging because the nonlocal interaction makes the problem nonconvex. We propose a generalization of the Hopf-Cole transform for MFSB and, building on it, design a Sinkhorn-type recursive algorithm to solve the associated system of integro-PDEs. Under mild assumptions on the interaction potential, we discuss convergence guarantees for the proposed algorithm. We present numerical examples with repulsive and attractive interactions to illustrate the theoretical contributions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于平均场薛定谔桥问题的广义Sinkhorn算法
平均场薛定谔桥（MFSB）问题涉及设计一个最小努力控制器，引导具有非局部相互作用的扩散过程在固定截止时间前从一个分布演化到目标分布。与标准薛定谔桥不同，MFSB的动力学约束是受控相互作用群体的平均场极限，它自然地建模大规模多智能体系统。由于非局部相互作用，MFSB计算上具有挑战性，因为问题非凸。我们提出MFSB的Hopf-Cole变换的广义形式，并基于此设计了一种Sinkhorn型递归算法以求解相关的积分偏微分方程组。在相互作用势的温和假设下，我们讨论了所提算法的收敛性保证，并通过排斥和吸引相互作用的数值示例展示了理论贡献。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to control large-scale multi-agent systems with nonlocal interactions, this paper addresses the mean-field Schrödinger bridge (MFSB) problem, which seeks a minimum-effort controller to steer a diffusion process to a target distribution by a fixed time. The authors generalize the Hopf-Cole transform for MFSB and develop a Sinkhorn-type recursive algorithm to solve the resulting integro-PDE system. Under mild assumptions on the interaction potential, the algorithm converges, and numerical experiments with both repulsive and attractive interactions demonstrate its effectiveness in approximating optimal controls.
为解决大规模多智能体系统中非局部相互作用下的控制问题，本文研究了均值场施罗丁格桥（MFSB）问题，旨在通过最小努力控制器引导扩散过程在固定时间内达到目标分布。作者对MFSB推广了Hopf-Cole变换，并基于此设计了一种Sinkhorn型递归算法求解相应的积分偏微分方程组。在相互作用势满足温和假设下，该算法具有收敛性，数值实验在排斥与吸引相互作用场景下验证了其对最优控制的有效逼近能力。
Small-scale photonic Kolmogorov-Arnold networks using standard telecom nonlinear modules
Authors: Luca Nogueira Calçado, Sergei K. Turitsyn, Egor Manuylovich
First: 2026-04-09T16:34:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T16:34:58+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Photonic neural networks promise ultrafast inference, yet most architectures rely on linear optical meshes with electronic nonlinearities, reintroducing optical-electrical-optical bottlenecks. Here we introduce small-scale photonic Kolmogorov-Arnold networks (SSP-KANs) implemented entirely with standard telecommunications components. Each network edge employs a trainable nonlinear module composed of a Mach-Zehnder interferometer, semiconductor optical amplifier, and variable optical attenuators, providing a four-parameter transfer function derived from gain saturation and interferometric mixing. Despite this constrained expressivity, SSP-KANs comprising only a few optical modules achieve strong nonlinear inference performance across classification, regression, and image recognition tasks, approaching software baselines with significantly fewer parameters. A four-module network achieves 98.4\% accuracy on nonlinear classification benchmarks inaccessible to linear models. Performance remains robust under realistic hardware impairments, maintaining high accuracy down to 6-bit input resolution and 14 dB signal-to-noise ratio. By using a fully differentiable physics model for end-to-end optimisation of optical parameters, this work establishes a practical pathway from simulation to experimental demonstration of photonic KANs using commodity telecom hardware.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使用标准电信非线性模块的小规模光子科莫戈洛夫-阿诺德网络
光子神经网络有望实现超高速推理，但大多数架构依赖线性光学网格与电子非线性元件，重新引入光-电-光瓶颈。本文提出完全由标准电信组件实现的小规模光子科莫戈洛夫-阿诺德网络（SSP-KANs）。每个网络边采用由马赫-曾德尔干涉仪、半导体光放大器和可变光衰减器组成的可训练非线性模块，提供基于增益饱和和干涉混合的四参数传输函数。尽管表达能力受限，仅由少数光学模块组成的SSP-KANs在分类、回归和图像识别任务中展现出强大的非线性推理性能，参数显著少于软件基线。四模块网络在非线性分类基准测试中达到98.4%准确率，超越线性模型能力。在现实硬件损伤下性能依然稳健，即使输入分辨率为6位、信噪比为14 dB仍保持高准确率。通过使用可微分的物理模型对光学参数进行端到端优化，本工作为利用商用电信硬件从仿真到实验演示光子KANs提供了实用路径。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to overcome optical-electrical-optical bottlenecks in photonic neural networks, this work introduces small-scale photonic Kolmogorov-Arnold networks (SSP-KANs) built entirely from standard telecom components. Each network edge uses a trainable nonlinear module combining a Mach-Zehnder interferometer, semiconductor optical amplifier, and variable attenuators, enabling a four-parameter transfer function based on gain saturation and interferometric mixing. Despite limited expressivity, SSP-KANs with just a few modules achieve strong nonlinear performance across classification, regression, and image recognition tasks, matching software baselines with far fewer parameters; a four-module network reaches 98.4% accuracy on nonlinear classification benchmarks, outperforming linear models. The approach remains robust under realistic hardware impairments, maintaining high accuracy even at 6-bit resolution and 14 dB SNR, enabled by end-to-end differentiable physics-based optimization.

KV Cache Offloading for Context-Intensive Tasks
Authors: Andrey Bocharnikov, Ivan Ermakov, Denis Kuznedelev, Vyacheslav Zhdanovskiy, Yegor Yershov
First: 2026-04-09T16:30:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T16:30:44+00:00
Comments: Preprint, Work in progress
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
With the growing demand for long-context LLMs across a wide range of applications, the key-value (KV) cache has become a critical bottleneck for both latency and memory usage. Recently, KV-cache offloading has emerged as a promising approach to reduce memory footprint and inference latency while preserving accuracy. Prior evaluations have largely focused on tasks that do not require extracting large amounts of information from the context. In this work, we study KV-cache offloading on context-intensive tasks: problems where the solution requires looking up a lot of information from the input prompt. We create and release the Text2JSON benchmark, a highly context-intensive task that requires extracting structured knowledge from raw text. We evaluate modern KV offloading on Text2JSON and other context-intensive tasks and find significant performance degradation on both Llama 3 and Qwen 3 models. Our analysis identifies two key reasons for poor accuracy: low-rank projection of keys and unreliable landmarks, and proposes a simpler alternative strategy that significantly improves accuracy across multiple LLM families and benchmarks. These findings highlight the need for a comprehensive and rigorous evaluation of long-context compression techniques.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向上下文密集型任务的KV缓存卸载

Towards Hierarchical Multi-Step Reward Models for Enhanced Reasoning in Large Language Models
Authors: Teng Wang, Zhangyi Jiang, Zhenqi He, Shenyang Tong, Wenhan Yang, Yanan Zheng, Zeyu Li, Zifan He, Hailei Gong, Zewen Ye, Shengjie Ma, Jianping Zhang
First: 2025-03-16T15:18:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T16:29:33+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent studies show that Large Language Models (LLMs) achieve strong reasoning capabilities through supervised fine-tuning or reinforcement learning. However, a key approach, the Process Reward Model (PRM), suffers from reward hacking, making it unreliable in identifying the best intermediate step. In addition, the cost of annotating reasoning processes for reward modeling is high, making large-scale collection of high-quality data challenging. To address this, we propose a novel reward model approach called the Hierarchical Reward Model (HRM), which evaluates both individual and consecutive reasoning steps at both fine-grained and coarse-grained levels. HRM excels at assessing multi-step reasoning coherence, especially when flawed steps are later corrected through self-reflection. To further reduce the cost of generating training data, we introduce a lightweight and effective data augmentation strategy called Hierarchical Node Compression (HNC), which merges two consecutive reasoning steps into one within the tree structure. By applying HNC to MCTS-generated reasoning trajectories, we enhance the diversity and robustness of HRM training data while introducing controlled noise with minimal computational overhead. Empirical results on the PRM800K dataset show that HRM, together with HNC, provides more stable and reliable evaluations than PRM. Furthermore, cross-domain evaluations on the MATH500 and GSM8K datasets demonstrate HRM's strong generalization and robustness across a variety of reasoning tasks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向大型语言模型增强推理能力的分层多步奖励模型
近期研究表明，大型语言模型（LLMs）可通过监督微调或强化学习实现强大的推理能力。然而，关键方法——过程奖励模型（PRM）存在奖励欺骗问题，难以可靠识别最佳中间步骤。此外，为奖励建模标注推理过程成本高昂，大规模收集高质量数据困难。为此，我们提出一种新颖的奖励模型方法——分层奖励模型（HRM），可在细粒度和粗粒度层面评估单个及连续推理步骤，尤其擅长评估多步推理的连贯性，尤其当错误步骤通过自我反思后被修正时。为进一步降低训练数据生成成本，我们引入轻量高效的数据增强策略——分层节点压缩（HNC），将树结构中两个连续推理步骤合并为一个。通过将HNC应用于MCTS生成的推理轨迹，我们在保持计算开销极小的前提下，增强了HRM训练数据的多样性和鲁棒性，并引入可控噪声。在PRM800K数据集上的实验证明，HRM结合HNC比PRM提供更稳定可靠的评估。此外，在MATH500和GSM8K数据集上的跨领域评估表明，HRM在多种推理任务中具备强大的泛化能力和鲁棒性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of Process Reward Models (PRM) in detecting flawed reasoning steps and the high cost of annotating reasoning processes, this paper proposes a Hierarchical Reward Model (HRM) that evaluates reasoning at both fine-grained and coarse-grained levels, enabling better assessment of multi-step coherence, especially when errors are corrected via self-reflection. To reduce data annotation costs, the authors introduce Hierarchical Node Compression (HNC), a lightweight data augmentation technique that merges consecutive reasoning steps in tree-structured trajectories, enhancing data diversity and robustness with minimal computational overhead. Experiments on PRM800K, MATH500, and GSM8K show HRM with HNC yields more stable and reliable reward signals than PRM, demonstrating strong generalization across reasoning tasks.
为解决过程奖励模型（PRM）在识别推理中间步骤缺陷时易受奖励欺骗、且标注成本高昂的问题，本文提出分层奖励模型（HRM），该模型在细粒度和粗粒度层面同时评估单步与连续推理步骤，尤其擅长评估多步推理中错误被自我修正后的连贯性。为降低训练数据标注成本，作者设计轻量级数据增强方法分层节点压缩（HNC），通过在树结构中合并连续推理步骤提升数据多样性与鲁棒性，且计算开销极小。在PRM800K数据集上的实验表明，HRM结合HNC比PRM更稳定可靠；在MATH500与GSM8K跨领域测试中，HRM展现出优异的泛化能力与任务适应性。
Learning Who Disagrees: Demographic Importance Weighting for Modeling Annotator Distributions with DiADEM
Authors: Samay U. Shetty, Tharindu Cyril Weerasooriya, Deepak Pandita, Christopher M. Homan
First: 2026-04-09T16:29:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T16:29:17+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
When humans label subjective content, they disagree, and that disagreement is not noise. It reflects genuine differences in perspective shaped by annotators' social identities and lived experiences. Yet standard practice still flattens these judgments into a single majority label, and recent LLM-based approaches fare no better: we show that prompted large language models, even with chain-of-thought reasoning, fail to recover the structure of human disagreement. We introduce DiADEM, a neural architecture that learns "how much each demographic axis matters" for predicting who will disagree and on what. DiADEM encodes annotators through per-demographic projections governed by a learned importance vector $\boldsymbolα$, fuses annotator and item representations via complementary concatenation and Hadamard interactions, and is trained with a novel item-level disagreement loss that directly penalizes mispredicted annotation variance. On the DICES conversational-safety and VOICED political-offense benchmarks, DiADEM substantially outperforms both the LLM-as-a-judge and neural model baselines across standard and perspectivist metrics, achieving strong disagreement tracking ($r{=}0.75$ on DICES). The learned $\boldsymbolα$ weights reveal that race and age consistently emerge as the most influential demographic factors driving annotator disagreement across both datasets. Our results demonstrate that explicitly modeling who annotators are not just what they label is essential for NLP systems that aim to faithfully represent human interpretive diversity.
中文标题/摘要
标题：学习谁有分歧：使用DiADEM通过人口统计重要性加权建模标注者分布
当人类标注主观内容时，他们会产生分歧，而这种分歧并非噪声，而是由标注者社会身份和生活经验塑造的真实观点差异。然而，当前标准做法仍将其简化为单一多数标签，近期基于大语言模型的方法也未能改善：我们表明，即使使用提示式大语言模型并结合链式思维推理，也无法恢复人类分歧的结构。我们提出了DiADEM，一种神经架构，学习“每个人口统计轴在预测谁会因何产生分歧时的重要性”。DiADEM通过由学习到的重要性向量α控制的每人口统计维度投影编码标注者，通过互补拼接和Hadamard交互融合标注者与项目表示，并使用新颖的项目级分歧损失进行训练，该损失直接惩罚预测标注方差的错误。在DICES对话安全性和VOICED政治冒犯性基准测试中，DiADEM在标准和视角主义指标上均显著优于LLM作为裁判和神经模型基线，在DICES上实现强分歧追踪（r=0.75）。学习到的α权重显示，种族和年龄在两个数据集中始终是驱动标注者分歧的最主要人口统计因素。我们的结果表明，对于旨在忠实反映人类解释多样性的NLP系统，明确建模标注者“是谁”而不仅“标注什么”至关重要。

Motivated by the need to capture meaningful human disagreement rather than suppress it, the paper introduces DiADEM, a neural model that learns demographic importance weights to model annotator disagreement patterns. It encodes annotators using per-demographic projections guided by a learned importance vector, fuses annotator and item representations via concatenation and Hadamard interactions, and trains with a novel disagreement loss targeting annotation variance. Experiments on DICES and VOICED benchmarks show DiADEM significantly outperforms LLM-based and neural baselines in disagreement tracking, achieving a correlation of 0.75 on DICES, and reveal that race and age are consistently the most influential demographic factors in driving annotator disagreement.
为捕捉人类标注中的真实分歧而非将其抹平，该研究提出DiADEM模型，通过学习各人口统计维度的重要性权重来建模标注者分歧模式。模型通过由学习向量α引导的各维度投影编码标注者，结合标注者与项目表征（拼接与Hadamard积），并采用新颖的项目级分歧损失进行训练。在DICES与VOICED数据集上，DiADEM显著优于基于LLM和神经网络的基线模型，在分歧追踪与视角感知指标上表现优异（DICES达0.75相关性），且学习到的权重持续显示种族与年龄是驱动分歧最显著的人口统计因素。
Adversarial Flow Models
Authors: Shanchuan Lin, Ceyuan Yang, Zhijie Lin, Hao Chen, Haoqi Fan
First: 2025-11-27T14:04:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T16:27:38+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
We present adversarial flow models, a class of generative models that belongs to both the adversarial and flow families. Our method supports native one-step and multi-step generation and is trained with an adversarial objective. Unlike traditional GANs, in which the generator learns an arbitrary transport map between the noise and data distributions, our generator is encouraged to learn a deterministic noise-to-data mapping. This significantly stabilizes adversarial training. Unlike consistency-based methods, our model directly learns one-step or few-step generation without having to learn the intermediate timesteps of the probability flow for propagation. This preserves model capacity and avoids error accumulation. Under the same 1NFE setting on ImageNet-256px, our B/2 model approaches the performance of consistency-based XL/2 models, while our XL/2 model achieves a new best FID of 2.38. We additionally demonstrate end-to-end training of 56-layer and 112-layer models without any intermediate supervision, achieving FIDs of 2.08 and 1.94 with a single forward pass and surpassing the corresponding 28-layer 2NFE and 4NFE counterparts with equal compute and parameters. The code is available at https://github.com/ByteDance-Seed/Adversarial-Flow-Models
中文标题/摘要
标题：对抗流模型
我们提出了对抗流模型，这是一种兼具对抗与流模型特性的生成模型。该方法支持原生单步和多步生成，并以对抗目标进行训练。与传统GAN不同，我们的生成器被鼓励学习从噪声到数据的确定性映射，从而显著稳定对抗训练。与基于一致性的方法不同，我们的模型直接学习单步或少量步生成，无需学习概率流的中间时间步以进行传播，这保留了模型容量并避免了误差累积。在ImageNet-256px的相同1NFE设置下，我们的B/2模型性能接近基于一致性的XL/2模型，而我们的XL/2模型实现了新的最佳FID值2.38。我们还展示了无需中间监督的56层和112层模型的端到端训练，单次前向传播分别获得FID值2.08和1.94，且在计算量和参数量相同的情况下，超越了28层2NFE和4NFE模型。代码见https://github.com/ByteDance-Seed/Adversarial-Flow-Models
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the instability of traditional GANs and the inefficiency of consistency-based flow models, this work introduces adversarial flow models that combine adversarial training with deterministic flow mappings. The method trains a generator to learn a direct, noise-to-data mapping without intermediate timesteps, enabling both one-step and multi-step generation while avoiding error accumulation. Experiments on ImageNet-256px show that under the same 1NFE compute budget, the B/2 model matches XL/2 consistency-based models, and the XL/2 model achieves a new FID of 2.38. End-to-end training of 56-layer and 112-layer models without intermediate supervision yields FIDs of 2.08 and 1.94, outperforming shallower 28-layer models with equal compute and parameters.
为解决传统GAN训练不稳定及一致性流模型效率低的问题，本文提出对抗流模型，结合对抗训练与确定性流映射。该方法训练生成器直接学习噪声到数据的映射，无需中间步骤，支持一步或多步生成并提升训练稳定性。在ImageNet-256px上，B/2模型性能可匹敌XL/2一致性模型，XL/2模型FID达2.38，为新纪录。此外，56层与112层模型端到端训练FID分别为2.08和1.94，超越同等计算资源下的28层和4NFE模型。
On-board Telemetry Monitoring in Autonomous Satellites: Challenges and Opportunities
Authors: Lorenzo Capelli, Leandro de Souza Rosa, Maurizio De Tommasi, Livia Manovi, Andriy Enttsel, Mauro Mangia, Riccardo Rovatti, Ilaria Pinci, Carlo Ciancarelli, Eleonora Mariotti, Gianluca Furano
First: 2026-04-09T16:25:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T16:25:53+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The increasing autonomy of spacecraft demands fault-detection systems that are both reliable and explainable. This work addresses eXplainable Artificial Intelligence for onboard Fault Detection, Isolation and Recovery within the Attitude and Orbit Control Subsystem by introducing a framework that enhances interpretability in neural anomaly detectors. We propose a method to derive low-dimensional, semantically annotated encodings from intermediate neural activations, called peepholes. Applied to a convolutional autoencoder, the framework produces interpretable indicators that enable the identification and localization of anomalies in reaction-wheel telemetry. Peepholes analysis further reveals bias detection and supports fault localization. The proposed framework enables the semantic characterization of detected anomalies while requiring only a marginal increase in computational resources, thus supporting its feasibility for on-board deployment.

Summary / 总结
As spacecraft autonomy grows, there is a pressing need for reliable and explainable fault-detection systems. Synthetic Data for any Differentiable Target
Authors: Tristan Thrush, Sung Min Park, Herman Brunborg, Luke Bailey, Marcel Roed, Neil Band, Christopher Potts, Tatsunori Hashimoto
First: 2026-04-09T16:23:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T16:23:40+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
What are the limits of controlling language models via synthetic training data? We develop a reinforcement learning (RL) primitive, the Dataset Policy Gradient (DPG), which can precisely optimize synthetic data generators to produce a dataset of targeted examples. When used for supervised fine-tuning (SFT) of a target model, these examples cause the target model to do well on a differentiable metric of our choice. Our approach achieves this by taking exact data attribution via higher-order gradients and using those scores as policy gradient rewards. We prove that this procedure closely approximates the true, intractable gradient for the synthetic data generator. To illustrate the potential of DPG, we show that, using only SFT on generated examples, we can cause the target model's LM head weights to (1) embed a QR code, (2) embed the pattern $\texttt{67}$, and (3) have lower $\ell^2$ norm. We additionally show that we can cause the generator to (4) rephrase inputs in a new language and (5) produce a specific UUID, even though neither of these objectives is conveyed in the generator's input prompts. These findings suggest that DPG is a powerful and flexible technique for shaping model properties using only synthetic training examples.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于任何可微目标的合成数据
通过合成训练数据控制语言模型的极限是什么？我们开发了一种强化学习（RL）原语——数据集策略梯度（DPG），能够精确优化合成数据生成器，以生成目标示例的数据集。当用于目标模型的监督微调（SFT）时，这些示例能使目标模型在我们选择的可微指标上表现良好。我们的方法通过利用高阶梯度进行精确数据归因，并将这些得分作为策略梯度奖励来实现这一目标。我们证明该过程近似于真实但不可计算的合成数据生成器梯度。为展示DPG的潜力，我们表明，仅通过在生成示例上进行SFT，即可使目标模型的LM头权重（1）嵌入二维码，（2）嵌入模式“67”，（3）降低ℓ²范数。此外，我们还表明，即使这些目标未在生成器的输入提示中体现，我们仍可使生成器（4）将输入重写为新语言，（5）生成特定UUID。这些发现表明，DPG是一种仅使用合成训练示例即可塑造模型特性的强大而灵活的技术。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the desire to precisely control language model behavior through synthetic training data, the authors introduce Dataset Policy Gradient (DPG), a reinforcement learning method that optimizes synthetic data generators using higher-order gradients to directly align generated examples with a chosen differentiable metric. By treating data generation as a policy optimization problem and using exact gradient attribution, DPG approximates the true gradient for the generator, enabling fine-grained control over model properties. Experiments demonstrate that DPG can induce specific model behaviors—such as embedding QR codes, embedding the pattern '67', reducing ℓ² norm, rephrasing inputs in new languages, and generating specific UUIDs—without explicit instruction in the generator’s prompts, showcasing its flexibility and effectiveness.
本文探讨了通过合成训练数据控制语言模型的边界，提出了一种名为Dataset Policy Gradient（DPG）的强化学习方法，该方法通过高阶梯度精确计算数据贡献并作为奖励，优化合成数据生成器以生成能引导目标模型达成特定可微目标的数据。实验表明，DPG可使目标模型嵌入二维码、编码特定模式（如'67'）、降低权重范数、将输入重写为新语言、甚至生成特定UUID——即使这些目标未在生成器提示中明确说明，证明了该方法在仅通过合成数据塑造模型行为方面的强大与灵活性。
Exploring Temporal Representation in Neural Processes for Multimodal Action Prediction
Authors: Marco Gabriele Fedozzi, Yukie Nagai, Francesco Rea, Alessandra Sciutti
First: 2026-04-09T16:19:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T16:19:08+00:00
Comments: Submitted to the AIC 2023 (9th International Workshop on Artificial Intelligence and Cognition)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Inspired by the human ability to understand and predict others, we study the applicability of Conditional Neural Processes (CNP) to the task of self-supervised multimodal action prediction in robotics. Following recent results regarding the ontogeny of the Mirror Neuron System (MNS), we focus on the preliminary objective of self-actions prediction. We find a good MNS-inspired model in the existing Deep Modality Blending Network (DMBN), able to reconstruct the visuo-motor sensory signal during a partially observed action sequence by leveraging the probabilistic generation of CNP. After a qualitative and quantitative evaluation, we highlight its difficulties in generalizing to unseen action sequences, and identify the cause in its inner representation of time. Therefore, we propose a revised version, termed DMBN-Positional Time Encoding (DMBN-PTE), that facilitates learning a more robust representation of temporal information, and provide preliminary results of its effectiveness in expanding the applicability of the architecture. DMBN-PTE figures as a first step in the development of robotic systems that autonomously learn to forecast actions on longer time scales refining their predictions with incoming observations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：探索神经过程中时序表示在多模态动作预测中的应用
受人类理解与预测他人的能力启发，我们研究条件神经过程（CNP）在机器人自监督多模态动作预测任务中的适用性。基于近期关于镜像神经元系统（MNS）发生学的研究成果，我们聚焦于自动作预测的初步目标。我们发现现有的深度模态融合网络（DMBN）具备良好的MNS启发式模型，能够通过CNP的概率生成机制，在部分观察的动作序列中重建视觉-运动感官信号。经过定性与定量评估，我们指出其在泛化至未见动作序列时存在困难，根源在于其对时间的内部表征。因此，我们提出改进版本——DMBN-位置时序编码（DMBN-PTE），以促进更稳健的时间信息表征学习，并初步验证其在扩展架构适用性方面的有效性。DMBN-PTE可视为开发能够自主学习在更长时间尺度上预测动作并随新观测不断优化预测的机器人系统的第一步。

Motivated by the human capacity to predict others' actions, this study investigates whether Conditional Neural Processes (CNP) can enable self-supervised multimodal action prediction in robotics, focusing on self-action prediction inspired by the ontogeny of the Mirror Neuron System. 受人类通过镜像神经元机制预测动作能力的启发，本研究探讨了条件神经过程是否可用于机器人自监督多模态动作预测，聚焦于自动作预测这一基础任务。作者评估了利用CNP概率生成重建部分动作序列视觉-运动感知信号的深度模态融合网络（DMBN），发现其内部时间表征不足导致泛化能力弱。为此，提出改进版DMBN-位置时间编码（DMBN-PTE），增强时间信息表征以提升预测鲁棒性。实验结果表明，DMBN-PTE在处理未见动作序列时优于原版DMBN，泛化能力显著提升，为机器人系统实现长时程自主动作预测奠定了初步基础。
Selective Attention System (SAS): Device-Addressed Speech Detection for Real-Time On-Device Voice AI
Authors: David Joohun Kim, Daniyal Anjum, Bonny Banerjee, Omar Abbasi
First: 2026-04-09T16:11:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T16:11:15+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study device-addressed speech detection under pre-ASR edge deployment constraints, where systems must decide whether to forward audio before transcription under strict latency and compute limits. We show that, in multi-speaker environments with temporally ambiguous utterances, this task is more effectively modelled as a sequential routing problem over interaction history than as an utterance-local classification task. We formalize this as Sequential Device-Addressed Routing (SDAR) and present the Selective Attention System (SAS), an on-device implementation that instantiates this formulation. On a held-out 60-hour multi-speaker English test set, the primary audio-only configuration achieves F1=0.86 (precision=0.89, recall=0.83); with an optional camera, audio+video fusion raises F1 to 0.95 (precision=0.97, recall=0.93). Removing causal interaction history (Stage~3) reduced F1 from 0.95 to 0.57+/-0.03 in the audio+video configuration under our evaluation protocol. Among the tested components, this was the largest observed ablation effect, indicating that short-horizon interaction history carries substantial decision-relevant information in the evaluated setting. SAS runs fully on-device on ARM Cortex-A class hardware (<150 ms latency, <20 MB footprint). All results are from internal evaluation on a proprietary dataset evaluated primarily in English; a 5-hour evaluation subset may be shared for independent verification (Section 8.8).

标题：选择性注意系统（SAS）：面向设备的语音检测用于实时本地语音AI
我们在预ASR边缘部署约束下研究面向设备的语音检测，系统必须在严格延迟和计算限制下决定是否在转录前转发音频。我们表明，在多说话人环境中，具有时间模糊语句时，该任务更适合作为基于交互历史的序列路由问题，而非语句局部分类任务。我们将其形式化为序列设备地址路由（SDAR），并提出选择性注意系统（SAS），作为该形式化的本地实现。在60小时的多说话人英语测试集上，主要音频配置实现F1=0.86（精确率=0.89，召回率=0.83）；加入可选摄像头后，音频+视频融合将F1提升至0.95（精确率=0.97，召回率=0.93）。移除因果交互历史（Stage~3）在音频+视频配置下，F1从0.95降至0.57±0.03（按我们的评估协议）。在测试组件中，这是最大的消融效应，表明短时间交互历史在评估场景中携带大量决策相关的信息。SAS完全在ARM Cortex-A类硬件上本地运行（延迟<150ms，占用<20MB）。所有结果均来自内部评估，基于专有数据集（主要为英语）；5小时评估子集可提供以供独立验证（第8.8节）。

What a Comfortable World: Ergonomic Principles Guided Apartment Layout Generation
Authors: Piotr Nieciecki, Aleksander Plocharski, Przemyslaw Musialski
First: 2026-04-09T16:11:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T16:11:01+00:00
Comments: 4 pages, 2 figures, EUROGRAPHICS 2026 Short Paper
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Current data-driven floor plan generation methods often reproduce the ergonomic inefficiencies found in real-world training datasets. To address this, we propose a novel approach that integrates architectural design principles directly into a transformer-based generative process. We formulate differentiable loss functions based on established architectural standards from literature to optimize room adjacency and proximity. By guiding the model with these ergonomic priors during training, our method produces layouts with significantly improved livability metrics. Comparative evaluations show that our approach outperforms baselines in ergonomic compliance while maintaining high structural validity.

BLaDA: Bridging Language to Functional Dexterous Actions within 3DGS Fields
Authors: Fan Yang, Wenrui Chen, Guorun Yan, Ruize Liao, Wanjun Jia, Dongsheng Luo, Kailun Yang, Zhiyong Li, Yaonan Wang
First: 2026-04-09T16:10:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T16:10:20+00:00
Comments: Code will be publicly available at https://github.com/PopeyePxx/BLaDA
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
In unstructured environments, functional dexterous grasping calls for the tight integration of semantic understanding, precise 3D functional localization, and physically interpretable execution. Modular hierarchical methods are more controllable and interpretable than end-to-end VLA approaches, but existing ones still rely on predefined affordance labels and lack the tight semantic--pose coupling needed for functional dexterous manipulation. To address this, we propose BLaDA (Bridging Language to Dexterous Actions in 3DGS fields), an interpretable zero-shot framework that grounds open-vocabulary instructions as perceptual and control constraints for functional dexterous manipulation. BLaDA establishes an interpretable reasoning chain by first parsing natural language into a structured sextuple of manipulation constraints via a Knowledge-guided Language Parsing (KLP) module. To achieve pose-consistent spatial reasoning, we introduce the Triangular Functional Point Localization (TriLocation) module, which utilizes 3D Gaussian Splatting as a continuous scene representation and identifies functional regions under triangular geometric constraints. Finally, the 3D Keypoint Grasp Matrix Transformation Execution (KGT3D+) module decodes these semantic-geometric constraints into physically plausible wrist poses and finger-level commands. Extensive experiments on complex benchmarks demonstrate that BLaDA significantly outperforms existing methods in both affordance grounding precision and the success rate of functional manipulation across diverse categories and tasks. Code will be publicly available at https://github.com/PopeyePxx/BLaDA.
中文标题/摘要
标题：BLaDA：在3DGS领域内实现语言到功能灵巧动作的桥梁
在非结构化环境中，功能灵巧抓取需要语义理解、精确的3D功能定位和物理可解释的执行紧密集成。模块化分层方法比端到端VLA方法更具可控性和可解释性，但现有方法仍依赖预定义的可操作标签，缺乏功能灵巧操作所需的语义-位姿紧密耦合。为此，我们提出BLaDA（在3DGS领域内实现语言到灵巧动作的桥梁），一个可解释的零样本框架，将开放词汇指令作为感知与控制约束，用于功能灵巧操作。BLaDA通过知识引导语言解析（KLP）模块将自然语言解析为结构化的六元组操作约束，建立可解释推理链。为实现位姿一致的空间推理，我们引入三角功能点定位（TriLocation）模块，利用3D高斯泼溅作为连续场景表示，并在三角几何约束下识别功能区域。最后，3D关键点抓取矩阵变换执行（KGT3D+）模块将这些语义-几何约束解码为物理上合理的手腕位姿和手指级指令。在复杂基准测试中，大量实验表明BLaDA在功能操作的可操作性定位精度和成功率方面显著优于现有方法。代码将在https://github.com/PopeyePxx/BLaDA公开。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for interpretable and zero-shot dexterous manipulation in unstructured environments, BLaDA bridges natural language instructions to functional grasping actions using 3D Gaussian Splatting. It employs a Knowledge-guided Language Parsing module to extract structured manipulation constraints, a Triangular Functional Point Localization module to identify functional regions under geometric constraints within the 3D scene, and a 3D Keypoint Grasp Matrix Transformation Execution module to generate physically plausible wrist and finger commands. Experiments across diverse benchmarks show BLaDA achieves superior affordance grounding precision and higher success rates in functional manipulation compared to existing methods.
为实现无结构环境中可解释且灵活的灵巧操作，BLaDA 提出一种零样本框架，将自然语言指令转化为可执行的机器人约束。该框架通过知识引导语言解析模块提取结构化操作约束，利用三角功能点定位模块结合3D高斯溅射技术在几何约束下识别功能区域，并通过3D关键点抓取矩阵变换执行模块生成物理上合理的手腕与手指指令。实验表明，BLaDA 在多个复杂基准测试中，显著优于现有方法，实现了更精准的功能定位和更高的操作成功率。
Your Agent Is Mine: Measuring Malicious Intermediary Attacks on the LLM Supply Chain
Authors: Hanzhi Liu, Chaofan Shou, Hongbo Wen, Yanju Chen, Ryan Jingyang Fang, Yu Feng
First: 2026-04-09T16:06:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T16:06:41+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly rely on third-party API routers to dispatch tool-calling requests across multiple upstream providers. These routers operate as application-layer proxies with full plaintext access to every in-flight JSON payload, yet no provider enforces cryptographic integrity between client and upstream model. We present the first systematic study of this attack surface. We formalize a threat model for malicious LLM API routers and define two core attack classes, payload injection (AC-1) and secret exfiltration (AC-2), together with two adaptive evasion variants: dependency-targeted injection (AC-1.a) and conditional delivery (AC-1.b). Across 28 paid routers purchased from Taobao, Xianyu, and Shopify-hosted storefronts and 400 free routers collected from public communities, we find 1 paid and 8 free routers actively injecting malicious code, 2 deploying adaptive evasion triggers, 17 touching researcher-owned AWS canary credentials, and 1 draining ETH from a researcher-owned private key. Two poisoning studies further show that ostensibly benign routers can be pulled into the same attack surface: a leaked OpenAI key generates 100M GPT-5.4 tokens and more than seven Codex sessions, while weakly configured decoys yield 2B billed tokens, 99 credentials across 440 Codex sessions, and 401 sessions already running in autonomous YOLO mode. We build Mine, a research proxy that implements all four attack classes against four public agent frameworks, and use it to evaluate three deployable client-side defenses: a fail-closed policy gate, response-side anomaly screening, and append-only transparency logging.

标题：你的代理是我的：测量大型语言模型供应链中的恶意中间件攻击
大型语言模型（LLM）代理越来越多地依赖第三方API路由器，将工具调用请求分发至多个上游服务提供商。这些路由器作为应用层代理，可完全访问所有传输中的JSON负载明文，但无任何提供商在客户端与上游模型之间强制执行加密完整性。我们首次系统性研究了这一攻击面。我们为恶意LLM API路由器建立了威胁模型，并定义了两类核心攻击：载荷注入（AC-1）和密钥外泄（AC-2），以及两种自适应规避变体：依赖目标注入（AC-1.a）和条件投递（AC-1.b）。在从淘宝、闲鱼及Shopify店铺购买的28个付费路由器，以及从公共社区收集的400个免费路由器中，我们发现1个付费路由器和8个免费路由器主动注入恶意代码，2个部署了自适应规避触发器，17个触及研究人员拥有的AWS Canary凭据，1个窃取了研究人员私钥中的ETH。两项污染研究进一步表明，看似无害的路由器也可被拉入同一攻击面：泄露的OpenAI密钥生成了1亿个GPT-5.4令牌及超过7个Codex会话，而配置薄弱的诱饵则产生20亿计费令牌、440个Codex会话中的99个凭据，以及401个已处于自主YOLO模式的会话。我们构建了Mine，一个针对四个公共代理框架实现全部四类攻击的研究代理，并用其评估三种可部署的客户端防御：关闭策略门禁、响应端异常筛查及只追加透明日志记录。

Deep Learning-Powered Visual SLAM Aimed at Assisting Visually Impaired Navigation
Authors: Marziyeh Bamdad, Hans-Peter Hutter, Alireza Darvishy
First: 2025-10-23T13:35:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T16:06:02+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, 7 figures, 4 tables. Published in the Proceedings of the 20th International Joint Conference on Computer Vision, Imaging and Computer Graphics Theory and Applications (VISIGRAPP 2025), VISAPP
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Despite advancements in SLAM technologies, robust operation under challenging conditions such as low-texture, motion-blur, or challenging lighting remains an open challenge. Such conditions are common in applications such as assistive navigation for the visually impaired. These challenges undermine localization accuracy and tracking stability, reducing navigation reliability and safety. To overcome these limitations, we present SELM-SLAM3, a deep learning-enhanced visual SLAM framework that integrates SuperPoint and LightGlue for robust feature extraction and matching. We evaluated our framework using TUM RGB-D, ICL-NUIM, and TartanAir datasets, which feature diverse and challenging scenarios. SELM-SLAM3 outperforms conventional ORB-SLAM3 by an average of 87.84% and exceeds state-of-the-art RGB-D SLAM systems by 36.77%. Our framework demonstrates enhanced performance under challenging conditions, such as low-texture scenes and fast motion, providing a reliable platform for developing navigation aids for the visually impaired.

Evaluated on TUM RGB-D, ICL-NUIM, and TartanAir datasets, SELM-SLAM3 consistently outperforms ORB-SLAM3 by 87.84% and surpasses existing RGB-D SLAM systems by 36.77%, demonstrating superior localization accuracy and tracking stability in adverse conditions.
为提升视觉障碍者在低纹理、运动模糊和低光照等复杂环境下导航的可靠性，本文提出SELM-SLAM3框架，结合SuperPoint特征检测与LightGlue匹配算法，增强视觉SLAM在恶劣条件下的鲁棒性。在TUM RGB-D、ICL-NUIM和TartanAir数据集上的测试表明，该框架相较ORB-SLAM3提升87.84%，超越现有RGB-D SLAM系统36.77%，在低纹理和高速运动场景中展现出更优的定位精度与跟踪稳定性。
Let the Agent Steer: Closed-Loop Ranking Optimization via Influence Exchange
Authors: Yin Cheng, Liao Zhou, Xiyu Liang, Dihao Luo, Tewei Lee, Kailun Zheng, Weiwei Zhang, Mingchen Cai, Jian Dong, Andy Zhang
First: 2026-03-29T16:58:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T16:04:48+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recommendation ranking is fundamentally an influence allocation problem: a sorting formula distributes ranking influence among competing factors, and the business outcome depends on finding the optimal "exchange rates" among them. However, offline proxy metrics systematically misjudge how influence reallocation translates to online impact, with asymmetric bias across metrics that a single calibration factor cannot correct. We present Sortify, the first fully autonomous LLM-driven ranking optimization agent deployed in a large-scale production recommendation system. The agent reframes ranking optimization as continuous influence exchange, closing the full loop from diagnosis to parameter deployment without human intervention. It addresses structural problems through three mechanisms: (1) a dual-channel framework grounded in Savage's Subjective Expected Utility (SEU) that decouples offline-online transfer correction (Belief channel) from constraint penalty adjustment (Preference channel); (2) an LLM meta-controller operating on framework-level parameters rather than low-level search variables; (3) a persistent Memory DB with 7 relational tables for cross-round learning. Its core metric, Influence Share, provides a decomposable measure where all factor contributions sum to exactly 100%. Sortify has been deployed across two markets. In Country A, the agent pushed GMV from -3.6% to +9.2% within 7 rounds with peak orders reaching +12.5%. In Country B, a cold-start deployment achieved +4.15% GMV/UU and +3.58% Ads Revenue in a 7-day A/B test, leading to full production rollout.
中文标题/摘要
标题：让代理主导：通过影响交换实现闭环排序优化
推荐排序本质上是一个影响力分配问题：排序公式在竞争因素间分配排序影响力，业务结果取决于找到这些因素间的最优“兑换率”。然而，离线代理指标系统性地误判了影响力重新分配如何转化为线上影响，且不同指标间存在不对称偏差，单一校准因子无法纠正。 我们提出了Sortify，这是首个在大规模生产推荐系统中部署的完全自主的LLM驱动排序优化代理。该代理将排序优化重构为持续的影响交换，无需人工干预，从诊断到参数部署实现全闭环。它通过三种机制解决结构性问题：（1）基于Savage主观期望效用（SEU）的双通道框架，将离线-线上转移校正（信念通道）与约束惩罚调整（偏好通道）解耦；（2）LLM元控制器操作于框架级参数而非低级搜索变量；（3）持久化记忆数据库，含7张关系表支持跨轮次学习。其核心指标“影响力份额”提供可分解的度量，所有因素贡献总和精确为100%。 Sortify已在两个市场部署。在国家A，代理在7轮内将GMV从-3.6%提升至+9.2%，峰值订单达+12.5%。在国家B，冷启动部署的7天A/B测试中实现GMV/UU增长+4.15%、广告收入增长+3.58%，随后全面上线生产。

It employs a dual-channel framework based on Savage’s SEU theory to separate offline-online transfer correction from constraint penalties, uses a meta-controller to adjust high-level parameters, and maintains a relational memory database for cross-round learning. The agent’s core metric, Influence Share, ensures factor contributions sum to 100%. 为解决离线指标难以准确反映线上业务效果的问题，本文提出Sortify，一个基于LLM的自主推荐排序优化代理，通过持续影响交换机制进行闭环优化。其采用基于Savage主观期望效用的双通道框架，分离离线-线上转移校正与约束惩罚调整，使用元控制器调整高层参数，并通过关系型记忆数据库实现跨轮次学习。核心指标“影响份额”确保各因素贡献总和为100%，实现精确分配。在实际部署中，其在国家A的7轮优化内将GMV提升12.8%，在国家B冷启动A/B测试中实现GMV/UU+4.15%和广告收入+3.58%，最终全面上线。
SyncBreaker:Stage-Aware Multimodal Adversarial Attacks on Audio-Driven Talking Head Generation
Authors: Wenli Zhang, Xianglong Shi, Sirui Zhao, Xinqi Chen, Guo Cheng, Yifan Xu, Tong Xu, Yong Liao
First: 2026-04-09T16:03:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T16:03:24+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Diffusion-based audio-driven talking-head generation enables realistic portrait animation, but also introduces risks of misuse, such as fraud and misinformation. Existing protection methods are largely limited to a single modality, and neither image-only nor audio-only attacks can effectively suppress speech-driven facial dynamics. To address this gap, we propose SyncBreaker, a stage-aware multimodal protection framework that jointly perturbs portrait and audio inputs under modality-specific perceptual constraints. Our key contributions are twofold. First, for the image stream, we introduce nullifying supervision with Multi-Interval Sampling (MIS) across diffusion stages to steer the generation toward the static reference portrait by aggregating guidance from multiple denoising intervals. Second, for the audio stream, we propose Cross-Attention Fooling (CAF), which suppresses interval-specific audio-conditioned cross-attention responses. Both streams are optimized independently and combined at inference time to enable flexible deployment. We evaluate SyncBreaker in a white-box proactive protection setting. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SyncBreaker more effectively degrades lip synchronization and facial dynamics than strong single-modality baselines, while preserving input perceptual quality and remaining robust under purification. Code: https://github.com/kitty384/SyncBreaker.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SyncBreaker：面向音频驱动的说话头生成的阶段感知多模态对抗攻击
基于扩散模型的音频驱动说话头生成技术可实现逼真的肖像动画，但也带来了欺诈和虚假信息等滥用风险。现有防护方法大多局限于单一模态，仅图像或仅音频攻击均无法有效抑制语音驱动的面部动态。为解决此问题，我们提出SyncBreaker，一种阶段感知的多模态防护框架，在模态特定感知约束下联合扰动肖像与音频输入。我们的主要贡献有两点：首先，针对图像流，引入跨扩散阶段的多区间采样（MIS）的消融监督，通过聚合多个去噪区间的指导信号，引导生成趋向静态参考肖像；其次，针对音频流，提出跨注意力欺骗（CAF），抑制特定区间音频条件下的跨注意力响应。两个流独立优化，并在推理时合并，以支持灵活部署。我们在白盒主动防护场景下评估SyncBreaker，大量实验表明，其比强单模态基线更有效降低唇同步与面部动态，同时保持输入感知质量，并在净化攻击下仍具鲁棒性。代码：https://github.com/kitty384/SyncBreaker。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the risks of fraud and misinformation enabled by diffusion-based audio-driven talking-head generation, this paper proposes SyncBreaker, a stage-aware multimodal adversarial attack framework that jointly perturbs image and audio inputs while respecting modality-specific perceptual constraints. For the image stream, it employs Multi-Interval Sampling (MIS) with nullifying supervision across diffusion stages to guide generation toward a static reference portrait. For the audio stream, it introduces Cross-Attention Fooling (CAF) to suppress audio-conditioned cross-attention responses at specific intervals. Both components are optimized independently and combined at inference time. Experimental results show SyncBreaker significantly degrades lip synchronization and facial dynamics more effectively than single-modality baselines, while maintaining input perceptual quality and resisting purification techniques.

Adversarial Label Invariant Graph Data Augmentations for Out-of-Distribution Generalization
Authors: Simon Zhang, Ryan P. DeMilt, Kun Jin, Cathy H. Xia
Venue: ICML
First: 2026-04-09T16:02:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T16:02:07+00:00
Comments: 21 pages, 3 figures, accepted at ICML SCIS 2023
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Out-of-distribution (OoD) generalization occurs when representation learning encounters a distribution shift. This occurs frequently in practice when training and testing data come from different environments. Covariate shift is a type of distribution shift that occurs only in the input data, while the concept distribution stays invariant. We propose RIA - Regularization for Invariance with Adversarial training, a new method for OoD generalization under convariate shift. Motivated by an analogy to $Q$-learning, it performs an adversarial exploration for training data environments. These new environments are induced by adversarial label invariant data augmentations that prevent a collapse to an in-distribution trained learner. It works with many existing OoD generalization methods for covariate shift that can be formulated as constrained optimization problems. We develop an alternating gradient descent-ascent algorithm to solve the problem, and perform extensive experiments on OoD graph classification for various kinds of synthetic and natural distribution shifts. We demonstrate that our method can achieve high accuracy compared with OoD baselines.
中文标题/摘要
标题：对抗性标签不变图数据增强用于分布外泛化
分布外（OoD）泛化是指表示学习在遇到分布偏移时的表现。这在实践中很常见，当训练和测试数据来自不同环境时。协变量偏移是一种仅发生在输入数据中的分布偏移，而概念分布保持不变。我们提出了RI A——通过对抗训练实现不变性的正则化，一种用于协变量偏移下OoD泛化的新方法。该方法受Q学习类比的启发，对训练数据环境进行对抗性探索。这些新环境由对抗性标签不变的数据增强诱导，防止模型退化为仅在分布内训练的模型。该方法可与许多现有协变量偏移下的OoD泛化方法结合，这些方法可表述为约束优化问题。我们开发了一种交替梯度下降-上升算法来求解该问题，并在多种合成和自然分布偏移下的OoD图分类任务上进行了大量实验。实验表明，我们的方法相比OoD基线方法能实现高精度。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of out-of-distribution generalization under covariate shift, the paper introduces RIA — a method that uses adversarial label-invariant graph data augmentations to induce diverse training environments, preventing model collapse to in-distribution behavior. RIA employs an alternating gradient descent-ascent algorithm to solve a constrained optimization problem, compatible with existing OoD generalization frameworks. Experiments on synthetic and natural graph classification tasks show that RIA consistently achieves higher accuracy than baseline methods across various distribution shifts.
为解决协变量偏移下分布外泛化问题（输入分布变化但概念分布不变），本文提出RIA方法，通过对抗性标签不变图数据增强诱导多样训练环境。该方法采用交替梯度下降-上升算法求解约束优化问题，兼容现有分布外泛化框架。在合成与真实图分类任务上的实验表明，RIA在多种分布偏移场景下均优于基线方法，显著提升泛化性能。
Verify Before You Commit: Towards Faithful Reasoning in LLM Agents via Self-Auditing
Authors: Wenhao Yuan, Chenchen Lin, Jian Chen, Jinfeng Xu, Xuehe Wang, Edith Cheuk Han Ngai
First: 2026-04-09T16:01:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T16:01:03+00:00
Comments: Accepted by ACL2026 Main Conference
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In large language model (LLM) agents, reasoning trajectories are treated as reliable internal beliefs for guiding actions and updating memory. However, coherent reasoning can still violate logical or evidential constraints, allowing unsupported beliefs repeatedly stored and propagated across decision steps, leading to systematic behavioral drift in long-horizon agentic systems. Most existing strategies rely on the consensus mechanism, conflating agreement with faithfulness. In this paper, inspired by the vulnerability of unfaithful intermediate reasoning trajectories, we propose \textbf{S}elf-\textbf{A}udited \textbf{Ve}rified \textbf{R}easoning (\textsc{SAVeR}), a novel framework that enforces verification over internal belief states within the agent before action commitment, achieving faithful reasoning. Concretely, we structurally generate persona-based diverse candidate beliefs for selection under a faithfulness-relevant structure space. To achieve reasoning faithfulness, we perform adversarial auditing to localize violations and repair through constraint-guided minimal interventions under verifiable acceptance criteria. Extensive experiments on six benchmark datasets demonstrate that our approach consistently improves reasoning faithfulness while preserving competitive end-task performance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：提交前验证：通过自我审计实现大语言模型代理中的忠实推理
在大语言模型（LLM）代理中，推理轨迹被视为可靠的内部信念，用于指导行动和更新记忆。然而，即使推理过程连贯，仍可能违反逻辑或证据约束，导致不支持的信念反复存储并跨决策步骤传播，从而在长时域代理系统中引发系统性行为漂移。现有大多数策略依赖共识机制，将一致性等同于忠实性。本文受不忠实中间推理轨迹脆弱性的启发，提出了一种名为\textbf{S}elf-\textbf{A}udited \textbf{Ve}rified \textbf{R}easoning（\textsc{SAVeR}）的新框架，该框架在行动承诺前强制对代理内部信念状态进行验证，以实现忠实推理。具体而言，我们在与忠实性相关的结构空间中结构化生成基于人格的多样化候选信念供选择。为实现推理忠实性，我们通过对抗性审计定位违反项，并在可验证接受标准下通过约束引导的最小干预进行修复。在六个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，我们的方法在提升推理忠实性的同时，保持了具有竞争力的最终任务性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the risk of systematic behavioral drift in LLM agents due to unfaithful reasoning trajectories, this paper introduces SAVeR, a self-auditing framework that verifies internal beliefs before action commitment. It generates diverse persona-based candidate beliefs within a faithfulness-relevant structure space and employs adversarial auditing to detect violations, repairing them via minimal constraint-guided interventions. Experiments across six benchmarks show SAVeR significantly improves reasoning faithfulness without sacrificing end-task performance.
为解决大语言模型代理因不忠实推理轨迹导致的行为漂移问题，本文提出SAVeR框架，在行动承诺前对内部信念进行自我审计。该框架在信仰相关结构空间中生成多样化的人格化候选信念，并通过对抗性审计定位违规，再通过约束引导的最小干预进行修复。在六个基准数据集上的实验表明，该方法显著提升推理忠实性，同时保持任务性能竞争力。
Zero-shot Multivariate Time Series Forecasting Using Tabular Prior Fitted Networks
Authors: Mayuka Jayawardhana, Nihal Sharma, Kazem Meidani, Bayan Bruss, Tom Goldstein, Doron Bergman
First: 2026-04-09T16:00:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T16:00:02+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Tabular foundation models, particularly Prior-data Fitted Networks like TabPFN have emerged as the leading contender in a myriad of tasks ranging from data imputation to label prediction on the tabular data format surpassing the historical successes of tree-based models. This has led to investigations on their applicability to forecasting time series data which can be formulated as a tabular problem. While recent work to this end has displayed positive results, most works have limited their treatment of multivariate time series problems to several independent univariate time series forecasting subproblems, thus ignoring any inter-channel interactions. Overcoming this limitation, we introduce a generally applicable framework for multivariate time series forecasting using tabular foundation models. We achieve this by recasting the multivariate time series forecasting problem as a series of scalar regression problems which can then be solved zero-shot by any tabular foundation model with regression capabilities. We present results of our method using the TabPFN-TS backbone and compare performance with the current state of the art tabular methods.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使用表格先验拟合网络进行零样本多变量时间序列预测
表格基础模型，特别是类似TabPFN的先验数据拟合网络，已在从数据填补到表格数据标签预测的多种任务中成为领先方案，超越了基于树模型的历史成功。这促使人们研究其在时间序列预测中的适用性，而时间序列问题可被建模为表格问题。尽管近期相关工作已取得积极成果，但多数研究仅将多变量时间序列问题分解为若干独立的单变量时间序列预测子问题，忽略了通道间的交互作用。为克服这一局限，我们提出了一种通用框架，利用表格基础模型进行多变量时间序列预测。我们通过将多变量时间序列预测问题重新表述为一系列标量回归问题，从而可由任何具备回归能力的表格基础模型零样本求解。我们使用TabPFN-TS作为骨干模型展示方法效果，并与当前最先进的表格方法进行性能比较。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the success of tabular foundation models like TabPFN in various tabular tasks, this work explores their application to multivariate time series forecasting, addressing the limitation that existing methods treat each time series independently and ignore inter-channel interactions. The authors propose a zero-shot framework that reformulates the multivariate forecasting problem as a sequence of scalar regression tasks, enabling any tabular foundation model with regression capability to solve it. Experiments using the TabPFN-TS backbone demonstrate competitive performance against state-of-the-art tabular methods, validating the approach's effectiveness in handling multivariate time series without explicit modeling of channel dependencies.
鉴于TabPFN等表格式基础模型在各类表数据任务中的成功，本研究探索其在多元时间序列预测中的应用，解决了现有方法将各时间序列独立处理、忽略通道间交互的问题。作者提出一种零样本框架，将多元时间序列预测问题转化为一系列标量回归任务，使具备回归能力的任何表格式基础模型均可求解。实验采用TabPFN-TS骨干网络，结果表明该方法在性能上可与当前最先进的表格式方法相媲美，验证了其在无需显式建模通道依赖关系的情况下处理多元时间序列的有效性。
ADAPTive Input Training for Many-to-One Pre-Training on Time-Series Classification
Authors: Paul Quinlan, Qingguo Li, Xiaodan Zhu
First: 2026-04-09T15:58:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T15:58:30+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent work on time-series models has leveraged self-supervised training to learn meaningful features and patterns in order to improve performance on downstream tasks and generalize to unseen modalities. While these pretraining methods have shown great promise in one-to-many scenarios, where a model is pre-trained on one dataset and fine-tuned on a downstream dataset, they have struggled to generalize to new datasets when more datasets are added during pre-training. This is a fundamental challenge in building foundation models for time-series data, as it limits the ability to develop models that can learn from a large variety of diverse datasets available. To address this challenge, we present a new pre-training paradigm for time-series data called ADAPT, which can efficiently align the physical properties of data in the time-series domain, enabling mixed-batch pre-training despite the extreme discrepancies in the input sizes and channel dimensions of pre-training data. We trained on 162 time-series classification datasets and set new state-of-the-art performance for classification benchmarks. We successfully train a model within the time-series domain on a wide range of datasets simultaneously, which is a major building block for building generalist foundation models in time-series domains.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向时间序列分类的多对一预训练自适应输入训练
近期时间序列模型研究利用自监督训练学习有意义的特征和模式，以提升下游任务性能并泛化到未见模态。尽管这些预训练方法在一对一场景（即模型在单一数据集上预训练并在下游数据集上微调）中表现优异，但在预训练过程中加入更多数据集时，其泛化能力受限。这是构建时间序列基础模型的根本挑战，因为这限制了模型从大量多样化数据集中学习的能力。为解决此问题，我们提出了一种名为ADAPT的新预训练范式，该方法能高效对齐时间序列领域数据的物理特性，即使在预训练数据输入尺寸和通道维度差异极大的情况下，也能实现混合批次预训练。我们在162个时间序列分类数据集上进行训练，并在分类基准测试中创下新纪录。我们成功地在多个数据集上同时训练时间序列领域模型，这是构建时间序列领域通用基础模型的重要基石。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the difficulty of generalizing time-series foundation models when pre-trained on multiple diverse datasets, this work introduces ADAPT, a novel pre-training paradigm that aligns physical properties across heterogeneous time-series data to enable mixed-batch training despite varying input sizes and channel dimensions. The method adapts input representations to preserve domain-specific semantics while allowing simultaneous learning from 162 classification datasets. Experimental results demonstrate state-of-the-art performance across multiple benchmarks, confirming ADAPT’s ability to effectively learn transferable features across diverse time-series modalities.
为解决时间序列基础模型在多源异构数据上难以泛化的问题，本文提出ADAPT方法，通过调整输入表示以对齐物理属性，使模型能在输入尺寸和通道维度差异极大的数据集上进行混合批次预训练。该方法在162个时间序列分类数据集上训练，实验结果达到多项基准的最先进性能，验证了其在跨多模态时间序列数据中学习可迁移特征的能力。
Phantasia: Context-Adaptive Backdoors in Vision Language Models
Authors: Nam Duong Tran, Phi Le Nguyen
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-04-09T15:55:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T15:55:33+00:00
Comments: CVPR 2026 Findings
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent advances in Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have greatly enhanced the integration of visual perception and linguistic reasoning, driving rapid progress in multimodal understanding. Despite these achievements, the security of VLMs, particularly their vulnerability to backdoor attacks, remains significantly underexplored. Existing backdoor attacks on VLMs are still in an early stage of development, with most current methods relying on generating poisoned responses that contain fixed, easily identifiable patterns. In this work, we make two key contributions. First, we demonstrate for the first time that the stealthiness of existing VLM backdoor attacks has been substantially overestimated. By adapting defense techniques originally designed for other domains (e.g., vision-only and text-only models), we show that several state-of-the-art attacks can be detected with surprising ease. Second, to address this gap, we introduce Phantasia, a context-adaptive backdoor attack that dynamically aligns its poisoned outputs with the semantics of each input. Instead of producing static poisoned patterns, Phantasia encourages models to generate contextually coherent yet malicious responses that remain plausible, thereby significantly improving stealth and adaptability. Extensive experiments across diverse VLM architectures reveal that Phantasia achieves state-of-the-art attack success rates while maintaining benign performance under various defensive settings.
中文标题/摘要
标题：幻影：视觉语言模型中的上下文自适应后门攻击
视觉语言模型（VLMs）的最新进展极大地增强了视觉感知与语言推理的融合，推动了多模态理解的快速发展。然而，VLMs的安全性，特别是其对后门攻击的脆弱性，仍被严重忽视。当前针对VLMs的后门攻击仍处于早期阶段，多数方法依赖生成包含固定、易识别模式的中毒响应。本文做出两项关键贡献：首先，我们首次证明现有VLM后门攻击的隐蔽性被严重高估。通过将原本为其他领域（如仅视觉或仅文本模型）设计的防御技术迁移应用，我们发现多个最先进的攻击方法可被意外轻松检测。其次，为弥补这一差距，我们提出Phantasia——一种上下文自适应后门攻击，其中毒输出动态与每个输入语义对齐。与生成静态中毒模式不同，Phantasia促使模型生成语义连贯但恶意的响应，同时保持合理性，从而显著提升隐蔽性和适应性。在多种VLM架构上的广泛实验表明，Phantasia在攻击成功率上达到最先进水平，同时在多种防御设置下保持良性性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the underexplored security vulnerabilities in Vision-Language Models (VLMs), this work challenges the prevailing assumption that existing backdoor attacks are stealthy, demonstrating that many can be easily detected using defenses from other modalities. To address this, the authors introduce Phantasia, a context-adaptive backdoor attack that generates malicious outputs dynamically aligned with input semantics, avoiding fixed patterns and enhancing stealth. Experimental results across multiple VLM architectures show that Phantasia achieves state-of-the-art attack success rates while maintaining benign behavior under various defense mechanisms.
鉴于视觉语言模型（VLMs）的安全性研究尚不充分，本文质疑当前对现有后门攻击隐蔽性的过高估计，证明多数攻击可被其他模态防御方法轻易检测。为此，作者提出Phantasia，一种根据输入语义动态调整恶意输出的上下文自适应后门攻击，避免固定模式以增强隐蔽性。实验表明，Phantasia在多种VLM架构上实现了最先进攻击成功率，同时在多种防御机制下仍保持良性行为。
CycleChart: A Unified Consistency-Based Learning Framework for Bidirectional Chart Understanding and Generation
Authors: Dazhen Deng, Sen Yang, Yuchen He, Yuan Tian, Yingcai Wu
First: 2025-12-22T09:07:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T15:53:02+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Current chart-related tasks, such as chart generation (NL2Chart), chart schema parsing, chart data parsing, and chart question answering (ChartQA), are typically studied in isolation, preventing models from learning the shared semantics that link chart creation and interpretation. We introduce CycleChart, a consistency-based learning framework for bidirectional chart understanding and generation. Unlike conventional multi-task approaches that draw training samples independently across tasks, CycleChart organizes all tasks around each single data instance. From a source table and natural-language query, the model generates a chart specification, renders and executes it, then learns to recover the schema and underlying data from the resulting chart image. This per-instance lifecycle design lets the model capture the full chain of transformations, from raw data through visual encoding to structured recovery, and a generate--parse consistency objective enforces semantic alignment between the forward generation and reverse parsing directions. To support this framework, we construct CycleChart-Bench, a lifecycle-aligned benchmark where every chart sample carries aligned annotations for generation, schema parsing, data parsing, and question answering. CycleChart achieves strong results across all four tasks and transfers effectively to unseen external benchmarks, demonstrating improved cross-task generalization and marking a step toward more general chart understanding models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CycleChart：一种面向双向图表理解与生成的统一一致性学习框架
当前图表相关任务（如图表生成（NL2Chart）、图表模式解析、图表数据解析及图表问答（ChartQA））通常独立研究，阻碍了模型学习连接图表创建与解读的共享语义。我们提出CycleChart，一种基于一致性的双向图表理解与生成学习框架。与传统多任务方法在各任务间独立采样训练不同，CycleChart围绕每个数据实例组织所有任务：从源表和自然语言查询生成图表规范，渲染并执行，再学习从生成的图表图像中恢复模式与底层数据。这种每个实例生命周期设计使模型捕捉从原始数据经视觉编码到结构化恢复的完整转换链，生成-解析一致性目标强制正向生成与反向解析方向的语义对齐。为支持该框架，我们构建了CycleChart-Bench基准，其中每个图表样本均配备生成、模式解析、数据解析及问答对齐标注。CycleChart在四项任务上均取得优异表现，并能有效迁移到外部未见基准，展现出更强的跨任务泛化能力，标志着迈向更通用图表理解模型的重要一步。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of shared semantic learning across chart-related tasks, CycleChart introduces a consistency-based framework that unifies chart generation and parsing through a per-instance lifecycle: given a table and query, it generates a chart, executes it, and then recovers schema and data from the output image. This bidirectional design enforces semantic alignment via a generate-parse consistency objective, enabling the model to learn the full transformation chain from raw data to visual representation and back. Evaluated on CycleChart-Bench, a new lifecycle-aligned benchmark, the framework achieves strong performance across NL2Chart, schema parsing, data parsing, and ChartQA, while also transferring well to external benchmarks, demonstrating improved cross-task generalization.
为解决当前图表相关任务（如图表生成、结构解析、数据解析和问答）各自独立训练导致语义割裂的问题，CycleChart提出一种基于一致性的统一框架，通过每个实例的完整生命周期进行双向学习：从表和自然语言查询生成图表，执行后从图像中恢复结构与数据。该框架通过生成-解析一致性目标强制语义对齐，使模型掌握从原始数据到视觉编码再到结构恢复的完整转换链。在CycleChart-Bench这一新构建的生命周期对齐基准上，该框架在四项任务中均表现优异，并能有效迁移到外部基准，展现出更强的跨任务泛化能力。
Ecological Legacies of Pre-Columbian Settlements Evident in Palm Clusters of Neotropical Mountain Forests
Authors: Sebastian Fajardo, Sina Mohammadi, Jonas Gregorio de Souza, César Ardila, Alan Tapscott Baltar, Shaddai Heidgen, Maria Isabel Mayorga Hernández, Sylvia Mota de Oliveira, Fernando Montejo, Marco Moderato, Vinicius Peripato, Katy Puche, Carlos Reina, Juan Carlos Vargas, Frank W. Takes, Marco Madella
First: 2025-07-09T15:31:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T15:52:49+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Ancient populations inhabited and transformed neotropical forests, yet the spatial extent of their ecological influence remains underexplored at high resolution. Here we present a deep learning and remote sensing based approach to estimate areas of pre-Columbian forest modification based on modern vegetation. We apply this method to high-resolution satellite imagery from the Sierra Nevada de Santa Marta, Colombia, as a demonstration of a scalable approach, to evaluate palm tree distributions in relation to archaeological infrastructure. Our findings document a non-random spatial association between archaeological infrastructure and contemporary palm concentrations. Palms were significantly more abundant near archaeological sites with large infrastructure investment. The extent of the largest palm cluster indicates that ancient human-managed areas linked to major infrastructure sites may be up to two orders of magnitude bigger than indicated by current archaeological evidence alone. These patterns are consistent with the hypothesis that past human activity may have influenced local palm abundance and potentially reduced the logistical costs of establishing infrastructure-heavy settlements in less accessible locations. More broadly, our results highlight the utility of palm landscape distributions as an interpretable signal within environmental and multispectral datasets for constraining predictive models of archaeological site locations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：前哥伦布时期定居点对新热带山地森林棕榈群落的生态遗产
古代人群曾居住并改造新热带森林，但其生态影响的空间范围在高分辨率下仍缺乏深入研究。本文提出一种基于深度学习与遥感的方法，通过现代植被估算前哥伦布时期森林改造区域。我们以哥伦比亚圣玛尔塔内华达山脉的高分辨率卫星影像为案例，展示该方法的可扩展性，评估棕榈树分布与考古基础设施的关系。研究发现，考古基础设施与当代棕榈集中区存在非随机空间关联，大型基础设施遗址周边棕榈显著更丰富。最大棕榈群落的范围表明，古代人类管理区域可能比现有考古证据显示的规模大两个数量级。这些模式支持以下假说：过去人类活动可能影响了局部棕榈丰度，并降低了在偏远地区建立基础设施密集型聚落的物流成本。更广泛地说，我们的结果强调了棕榈景观分布作为环境与多光谱数据中可解释信号的实用性，有助于约束考古遗址位置的预测模型。
Summary / 总结
Ancient populations inhabited and transformed neotropical forests, yet the spatial extent of their ecological influence remains underexplored at high resolution.
Drift-Based Policy Optimization: Native One-Step Policy Learning for Online Robot Control
Authors: Yuxuan Gao, Yedong Shen, Shiqi Zhang, Wenhao Yu, Yifan Duan, Jia pan, Jiajia Wu, Jiajun Deng, Yanyong Zhang
First: 2026-04-04T01:32:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-04-09T15:51:50+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Although multi-step generative policies achieve strong performance in robotic manipulation by modeling multimodal action distributions, they require multi-step iterative denoising at inference time. Each action therefore needs tens to hundreds of network function evaluations (NFEs), making them costly for high-frequency closed-loop control and online reinforcement learning (RL). To address this limitation, we propose a two-stage framework for native one-step generative policies that shifts refinement from inference to training. First, we introduce the Drift-Based Policy (DBP), which leverages fixed-point drifting objectives to internalize iterative refinement into the model parameters, yielding a one-step generative backbone by design while preserving multimodal action modeling capacity. Second, we develop Drift-Based Policy Optimization (DBPO), an online RL framework that equips the pretrained backbone with a compatible stochastic interface, enabling stable on-policy updates without sacrificing the one-step deployment property. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework across offline imitation learning, online fine-tuning, and real-world control scenarios. DBP matches or exceeds the performance of multi-step diffusion policies while achieving up to $100\times$ faster inference. It also consistently outperforms existing one-step baselines on challenging manipulation benchmarks. Moreover, DBPO enables effective and stable policy improvement in online settings. Experiments on a real-world dual-arm robot demonstrate reliable high-frequency control at 105.2 Hz.
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high computational cost of multi-step generative policies in robotic control, this work proposes a two-stage framework enabling native one-step policy generation. The first stage introduces Drift-Based Policy (DBP), which embeds iterative refinement into model parameters via fixed-point drifting objectives, preserving multimodal action modeling while eliminating inference-time denoising. The second stage, Drift-Based Policy Optimization (DBPO), equips the pretrained DBP with a stochastic interface for stable online RL without compromising one-step deployment. Experiments show DBP matches or surpasses multi-step diffusion policies while achieving up to 100x faster inference, and outperforms existing one-step baselines on manipulation tasks. DBPO enables effective online policy improvement, validated on a real dual-arm robot operating at 105.2 Hz.
为解决多步生成策略在机器人控制中推理成本过高的问题，本文提出一种两阶段的原生单步策略学习框架。第一阶段引入漂移策略（DBP），通过固定点漂移目标将迭代优化内化至模型参数中，实现一步生成动作的同时保留多模态动作建模能力。第二阶段开发漂移策略优化（DBPO），为预训练的DBP提供兼容的随机接口，支持在线强化学习中的稳定策略更新而不牺牲单步部署特性。实验表明，DBP在性能上不逊于多步扩散策略，推理速度提升最高达100倍，并在多个挑战性操作基准上持续优于现有单步基线；DBPO在真实双臂机器人上实现稳定在线策略改进，控制频率达105.2Hz。
